# THE SINS — scene construction and revision

Staged configuration, 2026-10-16 Honolulu. This is the single operating procedure for source preparation, construction, drafting, revision and release. It consolidates the existing doctrine; it creates no story event, acceptance, installation, power or new engine. The prior complete doctrine and replaced operating records are preserved in the candidate's historical recovery files.

## Authority and scope

Latest explicit author instructions control. Read MANIFEST.yaml, authority/Resolution_Register.json, state/Post_Chris_Restart.json and the newest available actually delivered matching-branch manuscript/checkpoint. The state owner labels the packaged endpoint as a dated evidence snapshot, not an automatically current cursor. Newer author-reported delivery is recorded at its actual confidence level. No particular episode is the focus or a prerequisite of this project-wide repair. A snapshot, requested PLAY, saved draft, planned card or remembered summary is not proof of an exact delivered episode. Do not restart, replay or roll back to a packaged cursor. Recover a missing manuscript through available existing records; never manufacture an original from a summary. Hold only actions whose material facts cannot be established.

Keep five information roles distinct: complete canon and explicit author locks; delivered evidence and its acceptance status; each actor's actual knowledge; conditional future plans; optional presentation/research examples. A character can act on a false belief when genuinely acquired. Audience knowledge, a research document or another actor's private knowledge does not become that character's knowledge. Unknowns remain unknown at the scope they affect.

Full character and mechanics owners remain intact. authority/ENGINE.md and canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md supply surviving source mechanics and presentation detail; older workflow directions, historical cursors, universal survival scheduling, technique restrictions or quotas in them cannot compete with this procedure. Explicit protections, immortality and the Frank rule still apply. Nonconflicting source requirements are retained. Original research is reused; investigate only an actual unsupported question.

## 1. Prepare the episode's sources and available choices

Resolve selected actors through routing/MASTER_SOURCE_INDEX.json and complete_sources/ACCESS_INDEX.json. A COMPLETE view is a route, not the full source. Open the full individual mechanics, psychology, voice and relationship owners it names, plus material shared systems, opposition and current overrides. Use routing/DEPENDENCY_GRAPH.json to distinguish rule/actor references from optional technique material, historical evidence and future-plan references. Do not recursively load the whole cast because names share a bundle. If a new actor or consequential mechanism enters during drafting, close that new dependency before using it.

Retrieve the most recent relevant actual manuscripts for continuation, voices, scene shape and callbacks. Exact manuscripts support passage comparisons; an attributed summary supports only the facts it actually reports. Record which kind was available. Read whole relevant logical owners in complete chunks; detect truncation and continue rather than treating a tool's partial result as a full read. Reuse unchanged fully read material already available in this session, identifying the source/version used. A byte hash proves identity, not comprehension.

Resolve baseline, acquisition, current availability, conditions and explicit removals together. Historical future/unearned headings cannot relock a power explicitly earned later. Broad power categories, mixing, assimilation or visual anatomy cannot restore removed functions or grant unapproved upgrades. Conversely, unknown acquisition choreography does not cancel an explicitly established acquisition. Check source snapshot, body/form, reach, resources/equipment custody, resistance, senses, movement, defense, healing and noncombat utility for the actual participants. Do not invent exact healing rates, costs, vulnerabilities or carried equipment.

Keep the existing short working brief source-backed: branch/version/evidence; listener question and circumstances; distinct actors' immediate wants and pressures; materially available options and their source resolution; likely choices and independent responses; strongest developing entertainment, plausible ending and consequences. Include concise pointers to complete owners, not substitute personality summaries. Separate settled rules, conditional author locks and optional tendencies. A conditional lock applies when its real trigger occurs; lack of a quota does not make it optional.

Challenge the proposed obstacle with the strongest feasible action, including swallowing, moving, hiding, leaving, refusing, investigating, disabling, sealing or cooperating. A materially decisive omitted option needs a credible actor-specific reason, lack of knowledge, resistance, cost or refusal. Omission is not a failure merely because an ability is rarely shown. Characters are not compelled to optimize, fight, explain their reasoning or exhibit their whole kit.

## 2. Construct from people, consequences and the season's current direction

Choose what particular people want, why those wants collide now, what they can actually attempt and what makes the interaction worth following. Let personality choose an action, let that action change circumstances, and let independent actors respond. If generic mercenaries could replace the Sins without changing the consequential choices, rebuild the construction from their full owners. Do not reduce Kaze to rescuer, Chris to irritated parent, Billy to storage, Rick to technician, Morty to conscience or Zid to a pratfall. Preserve all their contradictory, quieter and affectionate possibilities.

The Sins may initiate from profit, pleasure, freedom, revenge, equipment, territory, curiosity, pride or affection. Robberies, cons, intimidation, wagers, opportunistic alliances, retaliation and noncriminal enjoyment do not require an official assignment or a secretly evil victim. Helping is voluntary. Independent wants, refusal rights, situational leadership, unequal moral thresholds, shared spoils, public support and private disagreements affect conduct. No permanent commander, mandatory warning, righteous justification or remorse is imposed. A disagreement may change cooperation, a decision, cost, trust or later behavior; retain the resulting emotional residue without forcing arguments.

Use the existing current_reoutline, champion_consequence_register, crossover_deployment and thread_register in topic_sources/Season_3_Post_Chris_Rebuilt_Outline.json, with selected Darkin and duel/travel owners as relevant. Reconcile them against delivered evidence before treating a numbered placement as pending. Keep the season's chosen objectives, independent opposition, unresolved promises and possible payoff visible. Do not replace an unfinished main development with another default repair, acquisition or administrative outing. A repeated cargo objective or rescue pattern is not made fresh by changing names. Vary what the participants want, the pressure, choices and result; no mandatory twist, victim, fight or shock.

A returning thread needs a substantive action or decision that changes its position, not a mention, vague report or cameo. Preserve actual resources, injuries, geography, information carriers and transport. Other actors pursue their own motives during real elapsed time without omniscient reports, automatic victories or mandatory cutaways. If a detour displaces a selected development, update its reachable next action/access requirement and review window. No quota to feature every actor, thread or region in every episode. Preserve genuinely uncompleted selected encounters without guaranteeing access, survival or an outcome.

Fame is neither protection nor proof of weakness. If a valid decisive action ends a conflict, let it end and revise the plan. Powerful characters may win easily. Tension can remain in uncertain information, different desires, other people's agency, simultaneous objectives, loyalty, reputation or the choice of what to do with power. Do not impose an obligatory cost or punish every victory. Explicit author outcomes and protections retain their real scope.

## 3. Draft an experience worth hearing

Start at the first worthwhile action or interaction, not routine briefing. A scene can earn its space through pleasure, tension, relationship value, discovery or change; quiet life and substantial domestic comedy are legitimate. The whole episode must earn the established twenty-minute minimum. Estimate using actual word count and a stated assumed narration rate; do not claim measured audio runtime unless audio was produced and timed. If worthwhile material is short, develop a causally useful strand or rebuild the episode before expanding prose. Repeated banter, explanations, accounting, walking and prolonged decided fights are not duration solutions.

Black Mimir is the sole ordinary main narrator: personal, expressive, Scottish-and-Chicago, witty, profane, emotionally responsive and fallible within his knowledge boundaries. Billy alone narrates confirmed Specials. Character voices remain distinct during both danger and comedy. One opening episode title; no internal scene labels, compliance commentary or repeated Deadpool hijack. Genuine urgency can use normal expressive punctuation; avoid all-caps volume tricks or unreadable noise. Preserve source-owned pronunciation, including Mulenear and Yormungander, without a spoken pronunciation lesson.

Use the preserved A01–A20 and T001–T120 catalog selectively in response to the passage. All 140 may be used; preserve their provenance, with no technique quota, rotation or required twist. Fair misdirection preserves observable truth. Confusing staging is not mystery. Techniques can serve tension, humor, intimacy, surprise or clarity and may be unnecessary in an already effective passage.

Develop some humor through a particular person's commitment to an absurd premise, someone else's response and a changed situation or meaning. This is a possibility, not a beat formula. Savage roasts, grotesque comparison, physical comedy, humiliation, parody, gallows humor, petty competition, pranks, failed jokes and affection remain available through actual personalities. Profanity alone is not a joke. Avoid interchangeable quip queues and recycled setup/comeback/exit exchanges. Let people enjoy possessions, powers and wins. Do not automatically convert absurdity into medicine, administration or rescue logistics.

Callbacks need a changed target, cost, relationship meaning or payoff. Consult derived/PRESENTATION_MEMORY.yaml for evidenced patterns and its specifically scoped examples. Do not invent recurrence counts. No communal bathing setups or bath callbacks. All male Sins are straight; platonic crude jokes, teasing and affection remain allowed, with no male/male romance, sexual activity or erotic framing. Morty is never sexualized. These are unspoken boundaries, not defensive dialogue requirements.

Orient a first-time listener at a change of place, elapsed time, speaker or physical/psychic reality. Make decisive movement legible: actual positions, access, origin/exit, sight lines, held objects, body/form, intake, contact and consequence. Explain what the action needs through Mimir's voice without giving every newcomer a biography. Distinguish narrator explanation from information actually given to outsiders. Keep private BELLY arrangements private under their owners. Routine switching may be brief while real inversion, timing, control and one-exterior continuity remain intact.

## 4. Read the whole episode, correct it, then release

Finish the actual draft privately. Read it from beginning to end as a separate review after writing, reopening the artifact when available. Use complete sequential chunks and continue any truncated read. An outline, summary, favorite scene, checklist or hash is not full-text review.

During that read evaluate the five existing responsibilities together: actual capabilities/combat; narration/presentation; lore/living world; behavior/group; story/causality. Keep correctness and editorial judgment distinct in the same existing review record. Compare decisive actions to exact source provisions and availability resolutions. Check injuries and elapsed healing, access/resistance, protections, knowledge, custody, money, inventory, travel and the one-exterior rule. Healing works at its established scope; persistent consequences cannot invent an impairment or repair clothes, property or other people.

Read for the listener's experience: why the conflict happens now; particular choices that change it; an earned confrontation ending; distinctive speech and behavior; developed comedy and suspense; meaningful thread movement; repetition; action geography; emotional residue; pacing; TTS clarity. A convenient arrival, interruption or cooperation cannot replace a missing decision. Strong canon compliance cannot compensate for an empty scene, and an entertaining scene cannot justify an invented power or fact.

Identify the least worthwhile stretch. Cut, combine, compress or change its development if needed; if it earns its space, record the actual reason without manufacturing a defect. Correct every identified material error or weakness in this episode, including weak causes and endings. Follow changed actions through their consequences and checkpoint. Recheck affected earlier strengths as well as the repaired feature: a healing correction must retain meaningful action and aftermath; a voice repair must preserve motive and knowledge; a pacing cut must preserve causal clarity and earned length. Use the compact regression cases in derived/PRESENTATION_MEMORY.yaml when applicable, without inserting their situations into the story.

After corrections, read the COMPLETE revised episode again. Repair newly exposed problems and reread the resulting complete text before release. If the first full read genuinely needs no changes it can be final; no rewrite quota. Reuse valid research and focus source rechecks on affected dependencies. Routine repairs are already authorized by PLAY and do not need another “thoughts?” command or author approval. This is instruction-level editorial work, not software enforcement or a promise of perfection.

In the existing paired checkpoint/episode_record_contract retain concise evidence: exact version/hash, full-text reading coverage, source/provision and actual passage supporting a material resolution, actual defects and their repairs, particular choices, earned ending, weakest-span decision, relevant regression outcomes, unresolved issues, word count and assumed rate. A regression result is PASS/FAIL/NOT_RUN/NOT_APPLICABLE with evidence, never a default pass. Separate observed failure category: MISSING_EVIDENCE, RETRIEVAL, INTERPRETATION, DRAFTING, REVIEW or MAINTENANCE. Do not infer the original cause from a symptom alone. Passage examples illustrate standards; copying their dialogue or plot is not improvement. Narrative quality remains editorial judgment, with author feedback distinguished from assistant interpretation.

Do not release a known defective draft as verified. Rewrite only an unsupported dependent action where possible. Ask one narrow question only if essential unavailable information or an author choice truly prevents completion; never outsource routine review or freeze unrelated work.

## 5. Preserve, deliver and reconcile

Save the exact final reviewed manuscript and paired checkpoint before authorized PLAY. Verify actual receipts and manuscript SHA256; mark PREPARED_SAVED_NOT_YET_DELIVERED until the response exists. Render that saved text unchanged, with no file links in PLAY. Any subsequent prose edit requires review of the resulting final text. At the next interaction reconcile actual delivery and its version as DELIVERED_PROVISIONAL; author acceptance is separate. A failed save is reported honestly, not disguised as a backup or solved by silently generating another version.

Update only supported observed state changes and affected future prerequisites. Completed, pending, displaced and no-longer-possible developments remain distinguishable. A dead opponent loses ordinary rematches; a changed operation adapts; losses and gains change actual options. Do not invent substitute encounters or discharge required duels offscreen. Derived editorial memory records only actual observed patterns, feedback and unresolved callbacks; it is not character knowledge or canon. Retain original manuscripts unchanged when producing a revision.

WORK/QAPX/SAVE changes files only and advances no story time. This staged repair does not install sources, approve episodes, authorize PLAY or certify a future draft. Deliver changed physical source files and necessary routing only at the requested packaging stage; keep recovery history outside ordinary active retrieval. Resume the newest unfinished step without restarting completed research. Report completed work, remaining evidence limits and the precise next action briefly.


## Targeted fight construction and review refinement — 17 October 2026

Within the existing preparation and whole-draft review, use canon/characters/s37_kit_application.md#current-capability-retrieval. Do not add a second workflow. For each actual fighter, resolve current availability against full relevant owners before selecting tactics. Record source-backed perception/automatic responses, movement/access, offense/control, defense/recovery, resources and material combinations in the existing short brief. A category can be irrelevant; it is not a mandatory action or screen-time quota. A decisive omitted answer needs an actual character choice or mechanical reason, not a desire to prolong the scene.

Construct readable spectacle: establish the few spatial anchors needed to understand the action; show commitment and a physical route; resolve the opponent's real response; make impact and changed position/damage clear. The next meaningful action should follow the changed circumstances. Do not prescribe fixed phases or force a counter after a decisive hit. Use attack chains where useful, not disconnected named-move lists. When a system first matters, explain its immediate function through a concrete action/result in Mimir's voice; do not halt for a power catalogue. Distinguish imagined/fear imagery from changed physical ground when the listener needs that distinction.

Vary rhythm: brief anticipation, fast decisive motion, selective close detail at the consequential impact, and a readable result. Do not describe every finger movement at the same intensity. Establish real scale before destroying it and show moving mass, altered routes or skyline afterward, rather than merely calling an impact epic. Let spectacular victories stand without compulsory punishment, equalization or additional phases. Humor and character voice can intensify or puncture a beat but cannot freeze urgent action. Tactical combinations require each contributing mechanic's actual prerequisites, no new synergy power.

Different fighters should solve problems differently. Preserve Chris's armed improvisation and actual tools, Billy's independent predatory choices and body/terrain mechanics, Arthur's precision and practical violence, Daniel's drunken simian/weapon/ice/hair ingenuity, and Kaze's adaptive integrated systems and chosen brutality. These are prompts to retrieve full identities, not replacement personality summaries. Cooperative openings require real positions, awareness and agency. Never force everyone to contribute or distribute a shared power set.

During the whole-draft review, identify the concrete passage establishing (1) why they fight, (2) the decisive geometry, (3) the ability's access and actual effect, (4) the opponent's feasible response or why none was possible, (5) the payoff and changed state. Check whether a missing passive/automatic system falsely enabled surprise; distinguish detection from choosing to avoid damage. Check whether a fight is merely compliant but monotonous, confusing, interchangeable or padded. Repair that actual passage, then reread the complete revision under the existing procedure. Record mechanical checks separately from editorial judgment. Structural tests cannot certify hype or guarantee future performance. No compulsory minimum fight length, move rotation, named technique announcements or resurrection of dead roster members.


Approved portrayal integration — 17 October 2026 Honolulu
Within this same preparation/review workflow, retrieve canon/presentation/Season3_Approved_Portrayal_Patch_20261018.md when recovery, repairs, Chris/Kaze craft, Charles/Meatwad filming or TPP is involved. Apply its stale-condition, quick-repair, audio-orientation, camera-access and distinct-comedy checks during whole-draft review, not a separate process. Preserve 25–30-minute direction/earned minimum without padding. Planned camera discovery, repairs and healing remain to be enacted in story, not installed as current events.

SEASON 3 — NOXUS SOLO WITH KAZE AND BRIAR
WORK only. Delivered E20 unchanged; E21 unwritten. No travel, encounter or intimate scene generated.

BEGINNING: Start Kaze in Noxus, briefly accounting for his departure/travel from E20 Zaun/BELLY. Briar is the first woman he encounters; they have never met. Develop reciprocal flirting and an early mutual hookup, then adventures together. No previous shared memories, gifts or partner familiarity. No exact Noxian address or meeting is pre-awarded.

DIRECTION: A loose character/world-driven adventure. Kaze and Briar respond to desires, opportunities and consequences; other regions are available through real travel. Preserve all selected developments and version-specific sources, including cursed2018 Baldur and separate Ragnarok/Valhalla-era Kratos, but remove mandatory first/second fights and the fixed Ionia-next route. No episode, partner or fight quota; no outcomes already earned.

WORLD: Ensemble presentation pauses; world history, clocks and agency continue. Kaze can face consequences of his own and every other Sin's actual actions. Require witnesses, information routes, time, motives and means; no omniscient universal retaliation. Do not freeze debts or rewind time. Do not invent convenient offscreen gang missions, gear, sales or resolved obligations. Reconcile the single timeline at reunion.

CURRENT E20: Billy on Zaun service road; Kaze and other Sins inside BELLY; Briar placement/relationship superseded by latest first-meeting correction. Six stolen Purple Pain cases unsold; cash allocated; Daniel110 silver, Billy20 earmarked, former Briar20 share unassigned common cash. Heisenberg alive. TPP rent and actual last-known position/resources remain outstanding. No author WORK step moves anyone.

ROMANCE ON: proactive adult flirting, reciprocal suggestive dialogue, private jokes, recurring consensual non-graphic intimacy and aftermath. Old E16–20 Kaze/Briar relationship-dependent claims superseded; no assumed exclusivity, attraction, group participation or erased partner ledger. Sexual acts and erotic act-by-act dialogue stay off-page. Clear refusal stops the advance; later renewed interest is her choice. See full romance owner.

LIFE: Samira, Vi, Jinx, Caitlyn, Ambessa restored living; Isolde/Norra/Tristana living pins. Grayson, Sky Young and Cassandra Kiramman remain DEAD. Follow full life owner for broad coverage and exceptions. No erased history, duplicate gear, new resurrection power or automatic knowledge.

ROSTER: Female Runeterra list means CHAMPIONS, correctly mapped by lore affiliation; supporting women are separate reference. Regional ties do not establish location. Full crossover registry and inactive/version-gated women are distinguished in the review and audit.

PRESERVE: Seasons1–2, campfire Specials, E1–20, opening details, full current kits/removals, all other Sins' own matchups, TPP, Heisenberg, independent Rick/Morty, Deadpool and remaining season developments. Preserve storytelling repair, readable destructive combat, 25–30-minute target, whole draft review and exact-save rules. No PLAY until asked.

FIRST-MEETING AUTHORITY: state/work/Briar_First_Meeting_Author_Override_20261018.json. Historical manuscripts remain exact; unrelated world outcomes/consequences remain. No PLAY.

SEASON THREE — FULL SCENE SUBSTANCE, MATCHUP PORTFOLIO AND CONSTRUCTION OWNERS

Urgot; Galio; Malphite; Dr. Mundo.
Chris: Micah Bell with Bob Ross; adult 1914 Jack Marston; Eyehole Man; apparent Colm O'Driscoll / fictional Stephen Hawking; Mr. Nimbus. Chronological ordering is Eyehole, Nimbus, Micah/Bob, Jack, Hawking, not slot numbering. Jhin, Twitch, Ziggs, Corki, Aphelios, Tristana and South Park Christopher Reeve are no longer Chris's selected opponents; Patorikku is unassigned.

Arthur Morgan: Graves; Gangplank; Twisted Fate; Samira; Miss Fortune.

Frank Castle: Vayne; Lucian; Akshan; Kalista; Talon. These five are RETIRED FUTURE PERFORMANCE preferences after his selected final exit. Do not force them into the opening or revive him on page later. Camille and Renata Glasc were rejected for Frank.
There are 45 historical selected positions: 40 still eligible future positions plus five retired Frank positions. No new opponent or duel is created by the Busta/Brian arrival or Charles residency. Chris/Billy are distinct fighters while sharing one exterior. Kayn/Rhaast is one opponent slot with two minds. Divine/support relationships and Teemo remain source-owned, not deleted for a strict '1v1' label. Other long-term duels stay after Chris's opening arc when truly accessible; no guarantee all fit S3.
All regional gates remain: sealed Mordekaiser, unknown independent Aatrox/Fiddlesticks restoration, Zaahen's actual host/history and no Darkin automatic fusion. Twenty-three retained named Darkin exclude Varus/Valmar/Kai deployment; confirmed ancient dead remain historical. No artificial parity, contradictory snapshot, source resurrection or compulsory audience. Chris's selected Bob/Hawking endings and Eyehole near-death are explicit constructed future outcomes, not delivered facts.

  4. Nidalee: Single cougar-shapeshifting hunter and territorial protector; change between human/cougar is one opponent, and spear/traps/scent/traction have real consequences.
  5. territorial motives and scale are real, not a convenient imposed deathmatch.

LATEST AUTHOR-APPROVED S3 IMMERSION / HYPE ADDITIONS — N01–N14 (7 OCT 2026)
Status: ALL FOURTEEN concepts accepted as selective story-construction possibilities and world-reactivity guidance within the EXISTING fifteen cards. This is an approval of directions, NOT a guarantee each occurs or a lock on its cause, outcome, scene date, character survival, access or previously unwritten knowledge. Do not extend the season to service this list. Prior H01–H12 remain active. Same current endpoint: II.1 D173/R25. Next unwritten IV.1 D174/R26 only on PLAY/NEXT. No played episode revised by this addition.
N01 — PURPLE PAIN VERIFIED CIRCULATION / III–V: The meth–Shimmer product Heisenberg already sells has selected name PURPLE PAIN. When a real buyer, shipment or informant reaches the story, demonstrate a bounded temporary advantage, the dependent user's or worker's own wants, an aftereffect or material loss, and an actual supplier/business consequence. Heisenberg already dominates Zaun crime; do not replay his founding or imply he owns Noxus. Foreign provenance must be established, not inferred from purple coloration.
N02 — COUNTERFEIT PURPLE PAIN / conditional business counterstrand: A rival or opportunist MAY sell imitation product and damage reputation/quality expectations. Identify who could make, distribute and misrepresent it, and show actual circulation/customer perception before Walter knows or retaliates. Not an existing fact, universal recipe or mandatory subplot.
N03 — FAULTY ENEMY FIELD GUIDE / II–IV: A particular hostile witness, official or contractor MAY assemble real Sins observations mixed with false generalizations (especially Kaze's short anchor-swap versus supposed unconstrained teleport, or Billy's swallowing versus assumed digestion). Written copies, messenger delays, tactical changes and failures are real. The enemy adapts competently to its actual evidence and can be right about some limits. No gang access to its unseen plans by default.
N04 — MEAT CANYON/CATFISH PENITENTIARY SOCIAL ECOLOGY / recurring relevant interior: Established prisoners may develop actual grudges, bargains, hierarchies, barter, alliances, and conflicting theories based on what they hear through CLEAR MEAT. Their reactions to Chris's genuine RDR2 play may be comic, hostile or strategic. Do not invent prisoners, breach secure containment, reduce Chris to their universal warden or stage a prison break solely for flavor.
N05 — CHRIS/BILLY BODILY-CHOICE DILEMMA / active major operation: A real moment MAY require exterior precision guns versus Billy's actual swallow/access capability. The partners disagree/choose under the SAME single exterior body; valid time, agency, gear and physical positioning govern what is obtained or lost. Avoid artificial failures from their known capabilities. The switch may be brief, normal or cinematic in narration according to pacing.
N06 — IMMORTAL BASTION HISTORICAL MISREAD / IV–V: A person with real knowledge of Mordekaiser/Black Rose/Darkin history may interpret Kaze's current true Darkin/Black Marrow appearance through mistaken local categories. Knowledge needs a legitimate source or visible evidence, and their conclusion may be right or wrong. No automatically awakened god powers, Kaze/Aatrox merger, restored Aatrox identity or Mordekaiser revival.
N07 — SION MEMORY SHARD / V.4: During an actually possible source-compatible battle, a triggered fragment of Sion's former life MAY surface without restoring his whole predeath self, intelligence continuity or redemption.
N08 — PERSISTENT COMBAT SIGNATURES / all battles: Character-specific damage in buildings, wounds, missing cargo, metal and road structures persists. Attribution by later observers requires surviving material, skill and a real evidence chain, not a perfect supernatural crime scene readout. Destruction and healing remain separate.
He remains a nonverbal dog; masking and source-defined range/knowledge limits apply. No subtitles, language translation, convenient omniscient clue or repetitive dog bit.
N10 — KAZE'S UNPREDICTABILITY TACTIC TURNS REAL / Acts III–V: Kaze can consciously amplify Vaas-like unpredictable behavior as outsider-facing psychological warfare, then genuinely change what he wants under provocation, recognition, curiosity, amusement or a destructive appetite. His self-awareness is NOT self-control. This can spoil his own tactical advantage and produce lasting costs; no forced irrationality every scene or universal mind-reading of him.
N11 — CHRIS/DUTCH/ARTHUR RDR2 UNEASE / IV.4 or earned intimacy: Chris knows the fixed game later history Their responses to living Chapter-3 Dutch vary with actual deeds, charisma and treatment of Arthur. Dutch may learn the existence of the game ONLY from a real information route and legitimate BELLY admission/viewing; Arthur chooses disclosures. A possible awkward scene is NOT enacted here, the future game isn't a prophecy and the other Van der Linde gang members are not imported.
N12 — CHRIS'S COLLECTIBLES AS A REAL CHRONICLE / entire season: Use actual acquired objects (I.1 lightsaber, existing keepsakes, later legitimately captured items) as practical or emotional callbacks. Track holder, place, condition, provenance, powers and consequences. An unknown artifact doesn't auto-activate; don't acquire an object offscreen for an elegant ending.
N13 — YORMUNGANDER'S COASTAL ECONOMIC AFTERSHOCK / II–V coast strand: Actual fishermen, captains, ports, food supply and travel decisions change in response to the serpent's physical location and observed danger. Character rumors follow ordinary routes, may be exaggerated, and cannot warn the inland Sins instantly. Billy's old-predator boasting can respond, without claiming unsupported prehistoric rivalry. Thor/serpent remain independent.
N14 — UNEQUAL VALUE OF VICTORY/PLUNDER / II–V: A seized thing has distinct utility, market, strategic, personal and collectible value to actual actors. A supply choke point may be far worse than a large generic treasury loss. Chris, Kaze, Arthur, Daniel and others can dispute retain/sell/destroy decisions with real ownership/access and costs. Avoid inventing a named powerful artifact simply to resolve a later plot.
INTEGRATION PRIORITY: Lean first on N01/N03/N10 and valid N05/N07/N06 spectacle, then use N02/N04/N08/N09/N11/N12/N13/N14 where they naturally intersect existing active conflicts, real people and recorded assets. No extra episode, compulsory cameo, power unlock, resurrection, new seventh Sin, new game save, unearned knowledge, automatic faction hostility or granted future success. Original S3 I.1/I.2/I.3/II.1 and their separate corrected replays are unchanged. Preserve H01–H12 and separate uncommitted gang/SBU patch status.


AUTHOR-APPROVED HIGH-IMPACT SEASON 3 CONSTRUCTION — 7 OCTOBER 2026, 16:34 HONOLULU
Status: explicitly approved authoring/plot construction DIRECTIONS for existing unwritten Season 3 cards, not performed scenes, forced win conditions, fixed future casualties, source-world facts, or newly accepted episodes. These are now controlling intentions when circumstances support them. Actual outcomes require actors' earned abilities, access, motives, resistance, resources, time and witnessed events. If an earlier PLAY result makes a prospective setup impossible, preserve the result and adapt the idea's purpose locally. Do not revise delivered I.1–II.1 or override D173; the current 21-card master owns all future placement.

H01 — VALUE-CENTERED HEIST / IV.2: Identify and remove the particular stock, payroll, specialized weapons, machinery or transport capacity on which a responsible hostile operation materially depends. The Sins prefer valuable intact seizure when viable; Billy's existing swallowing/secure BELLY storage may strip whole accessible loads. A crippling loss must have demonstrated scale relative to THAT operation, with supply, wages, contracts, protection, workers or patrons reacting; no invented cargo, easy capture, mandatory company collapse or free external storage vulnerability.
H02 — PSYCHOLOGICAL AFTERSHOCK / II–IV: Material loss travels through partial reports into fear, status anxiety, suspicion, internal blame, defections or costly boss decisions. Kaze can sometimes amplify genuine uncertainty; Billy, Arthur and Daniel contribute according to distinct agendas and lawful scene access. Actual mistrust need not be planted or fully controlled by the gang. A frightened leader may learn and counter, not automatically self-destruct.
H03 — HEIMDALL'S CORRECT READ / IV.1: His intent anticipation works visibly through real evasion, counterpressure, Bifrost/sword/Realm Shift as available. Correctly sensing a sincere intention does NOT certify the speaker's claim as objectively true; independently examine any mistaken return-home/access information. Kaze's real and sometimes deliberately presented unpredictability is not a foresight-off switch, guaranteed surprise blow, immunity or infinite social insight. The encounter ends by valid physical reach and competing chosen objectives.
H04 — NO HEAD TO CUT OFF / IV.4 and onward: Outsiders may mistake Kaze for the permanent chief and negotiate, threaten or pay as if he can bind everyone. The gang's real refusal rights and independent acts expose that mistake. Freedom can cost a deal, income, coordination or trust; do not treat lack of hierarchy as effortless advantage, invent formal command, or turn Kaze into a reluctant permanent boss. Arthur/Dutch remains its own genuinely affectionate, independently judged relationship, not a universal leadership lesson.
H05 — HUMILIATION WITH ECONOMIC TEETH / V.1: A hostile patron attempts a public demonstration to restore status after observable losses; the test fails or changes only through actual choices and unequal available abilities. Contracted fighters, voluntary competitors and paying clients each decide what the sponsor's promises mean. The patron's loss of prestige has follow-through in clientele, protection, workforce or bargaining power. This is distinct from I.2's arena-prize event; not a guaranteed second tournament or compulsory death.
H06 — THE VANISHING HOLDING / V.2: An informed attacker expects to destroy the gang's exterior home or stock. If real access, timing and Billy's choice permit, Billy can swallow an entire holding/house or vital structures before they are destroyed. Legitimately stored property stays impenetrably secure through the switch. The attackers may still win LAND, public access, trading relationships, usable outside footing or other exposed stakes. Never conjure a BELLY breach or force the house to disappear merely for a visual trick.
Different Sins can choose different justified responses, with Frank's independent boundaries and Kaze's nonretaliation rule intact. an actual severe offense and responsible-faction notice would be needed for faction-scale retaliation even if that separate rule is later committed.
H08 — THE RANSOM NOBODY WANTS TO PAY / later conditional capture: If the story legitimately captures an individually culpable, personally compromised operative, wealth may make ransom possible but never supplies a captive by itself. Their actual employer, relatives and associates can refuse, betray or be unable to pay. Billy's real prison, admission, custody, release and credible/deceptive bargaining remain governed by their owners. Neither ransom, refusal, family guilt nor the final disposition is fixed here. The broader SBU ransom threshold remains separately staged.
H09 — SION / V.4: Their prospective clash is memorable for warranted mass, battle choices, weapon reach, form-specific mechanics, shattered terrain and durable road/bridge/supply consequences. Source power disparity may shorten the exchange; never invent Sion parity, immortality or a drawn-out survival phase to satisfy spectacle. Darius has independent military priorities.
H10 — WORLD SERPENT / COAST / V: Yormungander's chosen positioning, aggression or restraint and Thor's independent home pursuit change the economic/navigational reality of a SPECIFIC reachable channel or harbor. Shipping routes, escorts, insurance/supply terms and localized survivors respond through actual information routes. No obligatory rematch, unearned translation, sudden home portal, instant capital-to-coast transfer or last-minute rescue of the inland Sins.
H11 — PATRONS FRACTURE THROUGH CHOICE / VI.1–VI.2: Vladimir, Elise, Shang Tsung, LeBlanc/Black Rose and any other actually present patrons hold distinct appetites, assets and limits. As the Sins press a concrete shared capability, one patron's rational resource choice may betray another's expectation. Internal fracture must come from actual misaligned interests and observations; do not summon all named villains to one room, assert omniscience, or label every genuine defeat part of a superior secret plan.
H12 — CONTRADICTORY LEGEND / EVERY ACT: Track different true, false, incomplete or biased witness accounts. Some mistake Arthur for the chief, others focus on Kaze, Billy, Frank, or a particular violent choice. A known witness, actual carrier and receiving interest must change a contract, guarded route, price, pursuit or refusal. No instant universal renown, mandatory viewpoint roll-call, unreliable tale promoted into objective fact, or automatic Death Note identity discovery.

APPROVED CONNECTED CONSEQUENCE CHAIN (ROUTING, NOT GUARANTEED EVENTS): Acts II–III now carry the five Chris encounters and early domestic fallout, while existing Noxian consequences continue through real people. Act IV develops the disputed claim, substantial faction loss, Briar contact and Dutch family friction. Act V makes public humiliation, informed retaliation, Matt's actual return and Sion's route destruction consequential. Act VI attacks the bounded private operation whose patrons can fracture, with coast events independent and an earned price of autonomy. Each phase derives its information, money and access from the previous enacted phase, never from outline knowledge. If one route fails, preserve the defeat and adjust the next feasible action rather than restoring the same convoy, building or boss.

Nobody is spared just to meet a later card. Independent quieter friendship, food, gaming, collection, disagreement and rest must coexist with criminal brutality and actual victims' continuing choices. Older separate pre-/post-SBU Seven Sins staging remains UNCOMMITTED; this batch approves the twelve Season 3 creative applications, not the entire older SBU wholesale.


FULL CARD SUBSTANCE / CURRENT INDICES

S3 I.1 / CARD 01 — R0–4 — THE CLAIM [FULFILLED D148–152; original plan retained as historical guide]
DELIVERED EPISODE: actual outcome and replay/original versions are owned by Current_Continuity_Source. The planning material below is historical, NOT a requirement to redo the scene.
Scene focus / continuing behavior: Begin with arrival needs and the gang choosing how to settle in: food, usable work space, a place to keep what they brought. Wade's brief account of actual paid work is interrupted; Mimir's explicit first-person outrage reclaims narration. The company's evidenced Ambessa connection motivates the existing chosen takeover. During that action Chris finds, actually ignites and obtains the real lightsaber, his FIRST new S3 memorabilia item; the quiet delight afterward matters. Arthur's Dutch recognition is the sole secondary and need not become an instant binding offer. Coastal news reaches Billy through a real local consequence, beginning his grudge. Family life and curiosity precede a new mission list.
New-listener orientation: Establish Noxus as the empire they have sailed to, the inland capital approach and this specific freight holding after the sea/port/inland journey, and the company's evidenced Ambessa trade rather than requiring remembered Arcane politics. Wade is a displaced masked mercenary doing a particular paid job; Dutch is Arthur's old outlaw leader, whose former authority cannot simply resume. Show what a real lightsaber does through its ignition and Chris's recognition. Explain the coastal obstruction as received news, not shared narrator knowledge.
Identify Wade and Deadpool as the same person before switching names; clearly mark the brief change of narrator and Mimir's return. Explain Elsewhered once as displacement from another world. Keep the recap to facts needed for this takeover; do not recite every S2 death. Chris obtains a working energy-bladed weapon, not a Force power.
The Sins identify the private fighting company’s actual connection to Ambessa’s equipment/intimidation trade and choose a profitable act of retaliation. Kaze distinguishes the people responsible from Noxus as a whole, without becoming gentle toward the responsible crew. The takeover is enacted: short, brutal resistance where appropriate, seized usable stock, a real place/stake and somebody left with a loss. Establish who witnesses what.
Deadpool’s brief authorized narration hijack collides with a local who recognizes a consequence of Wade’s own paid work; Mimir resumes. Arthur’s meeting with Dutch is caused by an actual contact or recognition. Dutch is the sole Van der Linde newcomer; his source-world gang does not accompany him. Coastal news of the serpent changes a price or available route during the same operation.
Irreversible turn: the gang has taken something and can now be found through that act. A company survivor/creditor has a concrete reason to retaliate; residents are not unanimously grateful.

S3 I.2 / CARD 02 — R5–6 — WHO GETS THE APPLAUSE [FULFILLED D153–154/R5–6; original plan retained as historical guide]
DELIVERED EPISODE: actual outcome and replay/original versions are owned by Current_Continuity_Source. The planning material below is historical, NOT a requirement to redo the scene.
REQUIRED NEXT OPENING — S3 I.2, AUTHOR CORRECTION 7 OCTOBER 2026, 11:30 HONOLULU
Begin the next episode with a compact, recognizable first-person Mimir orientation to Noxus, before the main offer or conflict takes over. Target roughly 150–220 spoken words, integrated with the actual street/holding and capable of being shorter once the point lands. This is an opening requirement, not new episode prose or a new event.
Explain Noxus as an expansionist empire rather than merely a city or generic villain town. It prizes strength, ambition, demonstrated usefulness and contribution; strength includes strategy, skill and cunning as well as muscle. People from different backgrounds can rise, but that opportunity coexists with conquest, coercion, hierarchy and powerful interests. Imperial ideals are not a guarantee of fairness or every citizen's personal belief. Explain the current capital's martial, crowded, commercially active atmosphere through a few concrete sensory details grounded in the established research and actual location.
Briefly contrast Piltover's wealthy mercantile prestige, invention and polished upper-city presentation with Zaun's industrial undercity, pollution, ingenuity and survival pressures; contrast both with Noxian public pride in strength, military power and advancement through usefulness. These are orientation contrasts, not claims that every inhabitant shares one outlook. The listener should understand why unusual outsiders may find opportunity here and why visible violence or notoriety also attracts recruiters, competitors and authority. The Sins are in the inland capital's freight district, not a coast mislabeled as the capital.
Use Mimir's personality, natural profanity and a sharp observation where it fits, without a lecture, a list of every champion or an omniscient claim about what locals know. Connect directly into the existing I.2 scene. Underlying evidence: reference/research/Noxus_Cast_Fidelity_Research.md, reference/research/Runeterra_Travel_and_Noxus_Geography_Audit.md and topic_sources/Runeterra_World_and_Travel_Source.txt. Research already completed remains usable; do not restart it for this addition.

First-impression application: Use individual, source-supported reactions to the actual Sins present when the offer arrives; Kaze wears only samurai pants and BLACK Air Force Ones. Apply Ensemble_Scene_Application.txt without a reaction roll call. Preserve all existing prize, agency, combat and consequence requirements below. Use expressive exclamation marks where quoted dialogue calls for them.

Scene focus / continuing behavior: Let Draven's offer or interference reach the now-visible gang while they are enjoying or arranging the new holding; they need not go hunting for a tournament. Daniel's skill and recognition exchange with Tai Lung and Samira's independent appetite for danger shape the one prize conflict. A modest game/meal or saber-display moment can frame the decision without a full-cast joke relay. Settle actual gain/loss, witness knowledge and survival before reserving anyone for later.
New-listener orientation: Draven sells arena spectacle and public acclaim; Tai Lung is a displaced martial master seeking the recognition he believes he was denied; Samira is an independent gun-and-blade contractor drawn to danger. Introduce each through the same concrete offer or resistance, not three biography scenes. Name the prize and whose terms bind whom.
Listener-clarity application: Introduce Draven's arena role, Tai Lung's identity as a snow-leopard martial artist, and Samira's paid work through the immediate offer. Explain the actual prize and freedom to refuse before the fight. Recognition, employment and ownership are different stakes.
Draven tries to turn the gang’s public reputation into spectacle under his terms. Kaze sees the implied ownership and negotiates or rejects it; the Sins choose what they stand to gain. Tai Lung acts because recognition matters to him, not because a bracket needs another fighter. Samira’s competing work interferes with the same prize.
Fight focus: a real contest or collision with a definite stake, readable terrain and a supported endpoint. Tai Lung must use his martial skill; a Daniel encounter is a strong proposed matchup only if their objectives and actual kits make it occur. Daniel’s drunken coordination, anatomy and ice remain available; drunkenness is not a blanket foresight immunity. Draven’s axes and crowd craft are dangerous without creating parity with every Sin.
Consequence: a winner gains a specified purse/access/claim, the loser loses it, and a public account becomes exploitable. No automatic friendship after a good fight and no forced tournament arc.

S3 I.3 / CARD 03 — R11–12 — THE FIRST COLLECTION [FULFILLED D159–160/R11–12; original plan retained as historical guide]
DELIVERED EPISODE: actual outcome and replay/original versions are owned by Current_Continuity_Source. The planning material below is historical, NOT a requirement to redo the scene.
Scene focus / continuing behavior: Collection/enforcement comes to the holding through its actual public loss. Chris takes exterior control when his gun work is the right action, while his saber stays at its known custody unless he deliberately chooses it. Teemo has a spoken objection/input through real access. Jesse's worker choice and Dutch's proposed contribution remain independent. End with an ordinary recovery/game/food opportunity and the actual property division, not a compulsory emotional gag. Matt's R15 departure is prepared; dispatch is not remote receipt.
New-listener orientation: Distinguish the collecting company, local workers, and Darius's imperial authority. Jesse is a free traveling guest with practical process knowledge and an independent concern. Matt is the allied protector/investigator preparing a real voyage, not an unexplained teleporting messenger. State Teemo's bound voice route only as much as needed to understand the present exchange.
Listener-clarity application: Clarify collection here as enforcing a demand, not Chris collecting souvenirs. Name Darius as a Noxian military commander and state what his restriction changes. Introduce Matt Murdock/Daredevil as the same blind lawyer and investigator when either name is first needed; show relevant extraordinary senses through action. Identify Teemo as the conscious voice bound to Chris's gear, with no franchise prerequisite.
The company answers the initial loss through a claim on the gang’s new stake and an immediate attempt to enforce it. The Sins choose to strike the responsible operation while Dutch offers a joint profitable action. Dutch offers his own contribution for a negotiated share. Arthur and the Sins decide independently whether the deal serves them; Dutch cannot pledge their consent. The fight gives Chris a reason to own exterior control, with precision gun work and Teemo’s actual input, before Billy is useful for a different physical problem.
Jesse’s local concern connects an affected worker to the enhancement trade; a business report leaves through a real carrier. Heisenberg's response occurs only after that report or other established information can arrive, potentially in a later card; this dispatch gives him no instant knowledge of the scene. RDR2 downtime after the action shows Chris doing a specific foolish or emotionally charged thing; it does not erase the casualties or losses.
Payoff/cost: the Sins keep a valuable holding and Dutch receives his agreed share. A local source of company muscle/equipment is lost. Darius’s resulting restriction hits income and movement; the gang cannot both profit from the disruption and remain anonymous beneficiaries. Matt prepares his actual R15 departure from what he legitimately knows.

S3 II.1 / CARD 04 — R15–25 — WHAT HOME IS WORTH [FULFILLED D163–173/R15–25; original plan retained as historical guide]
DELIVERED EPISODE: actual outcome and replay/original versions are owned by Current_Continuity_Source. The planning material below is historical, NOT a requirement to redo the scene.

A supply failure or false safe-passage claim intrudes on the gang's practical needs. Chris's ingredient/repair judgment and Billy's voluntary hosting can matter; selected collection opportunities are incidental, not a hunt checklist. Separately and clearly on the coast, Thor's bounded home bargain and the serpent's own choice change the route. Thor family leverage recognition is available only after an actual meeting. Matt is at sea.
New-listener orientation: Place the gang's holding separately from the coast. Thor is a displaced thunder god searching for his family and a way home; his hammer is Mulenear when named in spoken prose. The World Serpent, Yormungander when named aloud, is an independent immense sentient being with an existing conflict with Thor. A broker's promise is a promise, not a working portal.
Listener-clarity application: Briefly distinguish Thor's displaced home from Noxus and identify the serpent's existing conflict with him. Make shore, open water and the inland holding separate locations. Explain exactly what the broker offers and why it might help, without declaring that a way home exists.
Matt leaves by ordinary passage at R15. Noxian scenes and Zaun scenes do not borrow his presence during the voyage. The Sins act on their new supply/access problem rather than attending another general briefing.
Thor accepts a bounded service because a patron offers a credible but uncertain lead toward home. The bargain has a real object of value; it is not a magic portal promise everyone inexplicably believes. In a separate coastal location, Jörmungandr makes a visible decision toward intruding vessels or activity in his path. His action changes the commercial route at issue. The gang hears about it only through an actual carrier or local consequence.
Consequence: a paid claim of safe passage proves false in action; a shipment/arrangement is lost or rerouted and an identifiable party bears the loss. The serpent does not obey a broker or become a convenient ferry. Thor’s need for family is now being exploited, and he has a reason to remain engaged.

S3 IV.1 / CARD 11 — R51–57 — HE SAW YOU COMING
APPROVED H03: Anticipation stays mechanically genuine; a read of intent is not proof the sensed person is right about an alleged way home. Kaze may deliberately menace but does not switch off foresight or obtain a scripted hit.
Scene focus / continuing behavior: One access collision grows from the failed promise. Kaze and Heimdall's disagreement concerns an actual claim, with anticipation succeeding before a supported limitation or decisive counter. Mimir separates remembered fear from observed facts. Chris's excitement about a saber is not Force mastery or a special immunity against Heimdall. No extra import-introduction scene.
New-listener orientation: Heimdall is a displaced enforcer loyal to Odin who can anticipate intentions; establish that ability through a successful physical response and explain its immediate effect. Odin's relevance is the allegiance/home he seeks, without a pantheon lecture. The access dispute has a tangible object and conflicting current claims.
Listener-clarity application: Identify Odin as the ruler Heimdall serves. Define anticipation by the intention he detects and the physical response it permits; reading a mistaken person's intention does not certify their information. Explain Bifrost or Realm Shift by the visible effect only when used.
Heimdall attempts to secure a person or piece of access relevant to his return to Odin, colliding with the Sins’ own accepted bargain. Show his anticipation through actual movement, counterplay and punishment of readable commitment where his perception and physical capabilities support it; do not guarantee a first-exchange win irrespective of the opponent or situation. Sword, Bifröst and Realm Shift add pressure; he is not an untouchable man standing still while everyone misses.
Proposed central confrontation: Kaze and Heimdall over incompatible claims, with a narrow objective that either can abandon only at a cost. Use current full kits. Do not script an arbitrary hit, a magic blindfold, a compulsory Draupnir imitation, or a mandatory equal duel. If one obtains decisive reach, resolve it. If they break contact, show the actual physical route and why the chosen objective matters more than pursuit.
Turn: his perception correctly detects an immediate intention but does not validate the other person’s mistaken information. The failed promise costs a concrete access/contact. Kaze sees that an apparently useful patron is trying to make the gang enforce someone else’s account. Neither fighter becomes the other’s friend by author decree.

S3 IV.2 / CARD 12 — R58–65 — THE ROAD BREAKS [CLIMAX II]
APPROVED H01/H02: Resolve the particular value-bearing hostile operation, its genuinely business-critical loss and ensuing feasible leadership/employment consequences; do not script an unverified complete empire collapse or a repeat anonymous convoy.
Scene focus / continuing behavior: The road conflict follows a concrete loss or chosen refusal; show what the gang was doing before it, then decisively remove contested capacity through actual seizure, capture or destruction. FIGHT / CONSEQUENCE CONTRACT
Every consequential fight needs a motive, a physical place, a knowable objective, actual current abilities/equipment, an outcome and a changed next scene. Use the quickest supported decisive action. An epic fight can be brief; an epic episode can contain several uneven fights, reversals and hard choices. Do not turn the requested set pieces into survival guarantees, forced pairings, repeated escape scripts or immunity tests. A protected protagonist need not remain unscratched, prosperous, respected or morally vindicated. No cost is invented solely because every power supposedly needs a downside.

'The World Serpent' and 'Thor's hammer' can vary reference naturally after clear identification. Preserve the exact OHL cue inside the requested spelling; this is not permission for shouted sentences. Kaze is unchanged. This is a text authoring rule, not a claim that a particular unauditioned TTS engine is guaranteed perfect.

Season 3 must be fully understandable to a new listener concerning EVERYTHING outside the already familiar Sins. Do not repeatedly reintroduce the Sins; orient their present place, action and consequences when needed. Noxus, native characters, imports, unfamiliar institutions, retained supporting characters, weapons and consequential abilities need enough context within PLAY to follow the immediate choice without a source file or franchise knowledge. No lore prerequisites.
At first relevant appearance, weave together the person's name, a recognizable role/relationship, their immediate motive and the visible behavior/gear that matters. Later appearances need only a brief reorientation if a long gap or changed allegiance makes it necessary. A listener must know why Arthur cares about Dutch; what makes a Noxian military commander different from a private patron; why Thor seeks home; why a bound voice speaks from Chris's gear; why a displaced fighter refuses a deal. Explain these through the actual scene, not a roll-call, full biography or source extraction timestamp.
The completed Noxian research must shape actual distinct dialogue, decisions, body movement, weapons and interests. Swain's courteous political pressure, Darius's civic/military conviction, Draven's audience hunger, Samira's thrill seeking, Riven's responsibility, Briar's curiosity/control, Katarina/Talon's divergent obligations and the individual Black Rose interests are not interchangeable 'Noxian villain' behavior. Consult the active individual's dedicated owner before writing consequential speech/action. Do not convert abilities into game-stat jargon or import an unresolved later development.
Silent release requirement: following the scene by hearing alone, can the listener identify who is acting, where, why, what the unusual thing does now and the actual outcome? Can they distinguish a rival's accusation from fact, private-company orders from imperial orders, and coast from capital/Zaun/ship? Fix a missing explanation locally in Mimir's own connected voice. This is a requirement for future actual prose, not certification that unwritten Season 3 has already been listened to or passed.


S3 CAPITAL ARRIVAL ANCHOR — QA RECONCILIATION
R0/D148 is arrival at the inland Noxian capital/its immediate freight approach, not first contact with the seacoast. The existing 28-day Piltover/Zaun-to-capital allowance includes sea passage, landing/transfer and the inland leg. Orient this briefly at I.1; the company holding is an inland freight/work site on that approach. Do not place ocean docks or the enormous serpent at the capital. Matt's two 28-day allowances likewise cover the total routes. The components' exact days remain uninstantiated until drafted; never add an instant coast-to-capital hop. Later coast/capital movement needs real travel and updates to dependent actions if delayed.

LATEST PACING LOCK — 7 OCTOBER 2026, 10:36 HONOLULU
User has explicitly held PLAY and is not finished directing the season. No episode starts during this work.
Every actual scene must sustain attention for a discernible reason: tension, a live choice, a relationship exchange, discovery, pleasure with a developing interaction, consequence or payoff. Purpose does not require a mission, a new clue or a changed personality in every quiet scene. Family breathing room remains; its enjoyable, revealing or affecting beat is the reason to stay. Ordinary activity alone is not a reason for extended screen time.
Enter near the meaningful action or exchange. Briefly bridge routine cooking steps, sorting, shopping, travel administration, loading, paperwork and repairs unless a particular moment genuinely matters. Once the scene's point lands, leave. Don't follow every mundane step just because a character is doing it. Don't pad to a runtime target.
Repeating a recap, argument, failed bargain, supplier meeting, ability explanation or joke without a changed meaning/outcome is waste. Combine or cut it. A callback needs new context, reversal or consequence. Do not expand the 36+26 interaction bank into obligatory skits or use every narration idea in each episode. Dialogue changes through actual answers, refusals, interruptions and actions rather than taking turns reciting traits.
Protect enough time for the strongest spectacle, clear fatal results, informed reactions and an earned emotional/comic beat; brevity must not blur those. For a quiet family moment, keep the distinctive interaction and compress the surrounding chores. For danger, act when the opening exists rather than prolonging it for banter. Let peaceful moments end peacefully when their purpose is fulfilled.
Before release, identify what each scene gives the listener and where that beat lands. Trim the material after it has landed and remove repetition between adjacent scenes. This is a local editing check, not a formula announced by Mimir, a compulsory plot twist or a claim about ungenerated episodes. The latest pacing direction qualifies earlier broad permissions to linger in ordinary activity.

SEASON 3 CONTINUITY, JÖRMUNGANDR AND THOR, AND TRAVEL REPAIR

Prospective application over the existing 24-card working S3 blueprint. The historical five-card S2 sequence was XIV.3, XV.1, XV.2, XV.3, XVI.1. All five are now delivered through the D120 finale; the earlier pre-delivery draft status is superseded. This file changes named carryover dependencies and existing S3 card functions; it does not restart the season, assert new delivered events or hard-lock 24 as a newly approved count.

SEASON 2 FINALE — XVI.1 D120
Keep the episode's existing payoff: aftermath of the D102 Hexgate climax, losses and repairs, Frank's voluntary membership, Jesse's choice, White's continuing empire and actual item custody, and the Sins' departure. Their earned resolution comes before the new arrival sequence.

In the final substantial sequence, Thor's source battle blow and the serpent's displacement become one brief rupture into open water beside a Noxian coastal roadstead. Both cross. The passage closes; source Asgard does not remain visible as a traversable road. This is the user-approved branch adaptation at the GOW event, not a second KNOCKTURNAL wave or Kaze's retired portal power.

Give the arrival a beginning, action and consequence. Nearby mariners first confront displaced water and the immense body; the ongoing conflict between Thor and the serpent produces a concrete local hazard. Rescue, ship damage and a blocked or newly dangerous approach matter to actual people. Avoid arbitrary casualty totals or wiping a named city off the map for scale. The scene must make clear where the two combatants go after the crossing.

Selected separation: the wounded serpent withdraws toward deeper water after an actual defensive response; Thor lands ashore and turns urgently to the lost passage and the need to return to his family/war. The source point supplies that priority, not a sudden generic mercy. No infinite paralysis, unexplained surrender, restored portal or automatic tracker prevents either from acting. Preflight the physical spacing and choice before writing a lethal outcome incompatible with the requested continuation.

Black Mimir recognizes what he can, with a personal reaction rather than a lore lecture. He does not know their exact memories from a glance and does not speak into the scene. End with a consequential choice or response—for example the shore authority closing the affected approach while Thor demands someone who can account for the vanished passage—and the serpent alive in the local sea. Do not substitute a remote silhouette or a title card for the arrival.

TEMPORAL CONSEQUENCES
Thor and Jörmungandr's local residence begins D120. Under the retained default Sins arrival D148/R0, they have had 28 days in Runeterra by R0, not the whole earlier season. Those days permit limited encounters and consequences, not a fully invented offscreen career. Any specific shelter, deal, report, confrontation or relocation becomes true only through the actual prepared/delivered causal record.

Other crossover newcomers retain their independently selected onset. Heimdall did not suddenly travel in the same rupture. No extra Thor or older serpent remains behind in an earlier Noxian state. The original GOW chronology explains the source reference; this branch does not require rewriting the older Lake of Nine story or resolving a multiverse paradox on-page.

SEASON 3 — INTEGRATE INTO EXISTING ACTION
Opening / existing cards 1–3: a real local aftermath or received coastal account changes port access, freight availability, insurance/escort price or a broker's claim. Show one pertinent consequence during the Sins' existing arrival and reputation conflict, not a separate parade of updates. Accounts can misidentify the arrivals. News of the Sins and news of the serpent are distinct evidence chains.

Existing cards 4–6: connect the coastal disruption to the freight or property at issue in the gang's chosen paid work. Include a real Jörmungandr decision in an affected coastal reach—refusing intrusion, allowing a vessel through after its crew changes course, or confronting an attempted claim—so he has active presence before the next Thor payoff. Select the action that follows the resolved local situation; do not treat these alternatives as three mandatory scenes or an automatic translation breakthrough.

Existing card 7, “A promise of home”: preserve Thor's limited bargain for access to travel knowledge. It now grows from the D120 arrival and an identifiable local contact, not months of pre-onset integration. A genuine archive or research lead can be useful while insufficient to open a route. The Sins' property dispute intersects that access. Mimir need not repeat his first recognition; this is a later encounter with someone the audience has met.

Middle S3: the serpent's choices and local reactions alter the same coast/freight strand. An attempt to divert shipping, sell a false safe-passage claim or recruit Thor to remove him must be enacted by an actor with access and motive. Neither Jörmungandr nor Thor is automatically employed by the private works. Native rescue, negotiation and resistance remain meaningful. Avoid guaranteeing a fight with Nautilus, Fizz, Volibear or any other named figure solely because of thematic resemblance.

Existing card 18, “What each one wants”: preserve Thor reclaiming/seizing the promised travel material after learning it offers no verified way home, while the Sins recover their diverted carriage. His family/home motive remains his own. Tie any affected coastal movement to the serpent's actual position and behavior; no coincidental appearance to solve the carriage problem. Thor's refusal to be permanently owned can change a route without destroying the entire political conflict.

Existing cards 21–24: the coastal situation must have a paid-off consequence in the selected operation or its aftermath. Establish whether particular access has reopened, been rerouted or remains dangerous, and what the serpent chooses next within that resolved situation. Do not silently kill, capture, tame or send him home to clean the outline. The user requested a continuing S3 presence; a final disposition should follow actual play and later direction. He need not become the season's universal final boss.

NECESSARY TRANSPORT REPAIR — REMOVED KAZE PORTALS
The old S3 blueprint still relied on Kaze taking Matt to Zaun at R15 and returning him with a witness at R54. Those two prospective uses conflict with the already approved power deletion. They are now replaced with the existing ordinary 28-day route allowance; no new portal provider is invented.

Selected working calendar: Matt remains Noxus R0–14, departs R15, reaches Piltover/Zaun about R43, completes the decisive local corroboration/protection work R43–53, departs with the consenting source at R54 and returns to the Noxian capital about R82. R dates are relative to the actual Sins arrival. These are prospective journey allowances, subject to real disruptions, not fixed source-certified passage guarantees.

Affected card repairs:
- Card 5 R15: the departure is a real journey. Established Zaun investigators pursue the local pending handover using their own evidence. Before Matt leaves, he hands off the information actually available; no instant receipt abroad is assumed.
- Card 9: native Zaun investigators preserve the case opportunity while Matt is traveling. He cannot perform their local interceptions remotely. The Maul/Riddick/Sins freight action keeps its own objective.
- Card 11 R43–47: Matt's actual arrival leads directly into the planned corroboration and living-source work. The native investigators' preparatory work matters rather than being erased when he arrives.
- Card 12 R49–53: keep settling the threatened coworkers and earned-pay obligation, then the witness's voluntary decision to travel.
- Card 13 R54: return voyage begins; no instantaneous witness appearance. Arthur and Dutch's disagreement uses Jesse's already established local findings, the actual bargain terms and Arthur/Dutch’s own evidence. It does not borrow the unseen Zaun witness account.
- Card 14: Jesse and an actually available local ally handle the Noxian transfer; Matt is aboard the return journey. Choose the ally from established participants for a concrete motive, not an unexplained duplicate.
- Card 16 R67–80: the Sins defend their base; witness protection in Noxus rests with Jesse and available allies. Matt's traveling source is not placed in the local attack.
- Card 19 R82: actual return and its surviving source/corroboration now help cause the recovery attempt and collision. Remove the old assertion that the source has been in Noxus since R54. Subsequent cards can use both people when physically available.

Do not fill either voyage with repetitive travel episodes. Time can pass through the other already active threads. If arrival is delayed, adjust the unfixed interception/rendezvous rather than teleport the witness or pretend a departed carrier delivered immediately.

APPROVED S3 COAST / CHARACTER CARRYOVER
II.1 already established why Thor accompanied the Red Gull and armed ships: shipping agent Serrat demonstrated matching tablet fragments with recognizable home-world runes and promised inland collector access in exchange for escort of ONE crossing. The fragments prove a possible trail, not a verified return path. Yormungander recognized Thor as his enemy; he watched approaching ships without automatically attacking, then an armed escort fired a chained harpoon near his jaw. He destroyed armed capacity, constrained the approach, let a small skiff escape, then remained an independent living serpent. Thor's hammer action killed the escort foreman and preserved some sailors; Serrat survived injured, with a demanded inland guide obligation. Do not add a second fight to justify this one. Their actual reports and carrier delay, not Mimir's narrator knowledge, determine what the Sins learn. Keep separate distant coast, Noxian capital and Matt's travel.

Preserve Frank's earned voluntary membership, the gang's chosen independence, Jesse's own choices, all character speech/interaction updates, and injury/property/reputation consequences. These new crossovers do not compress the Sins into spectators or replace the Noxian native cast.

  ],

    [
      5,

      7,

      10,
      20,

      37,
      44,
      46
    ],
    "Audible independent Omega Teemo through actual weapon/listener routes. He gives a valid warning, can disagree about Chris's handling and reacts to grief without turning into a scanner or resurrected body."
  ],
  "Frank Castle": [
    [
      5,
      6,
      7,
      8,
      9,
      10
    ],
    "Remains enhanced, independently competent and a Sin through the opening. Final exact single-line decision at III.4, already absent next morning. No later on-page action or revived retired duels. This is a complete selected arc, not an omitted character."
  ],
  "Heisenberg": [
    [
      18,
      21,
      24,
      33,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Already dominant in Zaun. Counterfeit scandal threatens quality and control; he uses a real notebook route where eligible, pays 47, loses a supplier and keeps an empire with opposition. No origin restart or automatic dominion over Noxus."
  ],
  "Matt Murdock": [
    [
      8,
      16,
      18,
      21,
      46
    ],
    "Ordinary voyage reaches Zaun R43, returns R82. Witnesses choose disclosure; he carries limited evidence. After the return he pursues his own case locally, without becoming a perpetual Sin or recalling departed Frank to perform it."
  ],
  "Jesse Pinkman": [
    [
      4,
      8,
      16,
      18,
      19,
      21,
      46
    ],
    "Practical competence and refusal have consequences. He distinguishes a genuine product from counterfeit evidence, protects an actual witness choice and refuses coercive recruitment. He has time away that is not treated as suspicious disobedience."
  ],
  "Agent 47": [
    [
      18,
      21,
      33,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Reconnaissance, independent contract scope and information provenance remain visible. He identifies an accountable maker, refuses an employer's unsupported wider target list and gets paid for actual work rather than divine knowledge."
  ],
  "Ryuk": [
    [
      18,
      21,
      33,
      46
    ],
    "Autonomous observer of the existing notebook holder. His amusement exposes White's choices; he does not fetch names, control users, guarantee ownership or become a useful moral adviser."
  ],
  "Thor": [
    [
      4,
      16,
      18,
      27,
      33,
      43,
      45,
      46
    ],
    "Tracks the surviving tablet and a real route-home claim while the coast changes around him. His actions can protect people or punish deception without making him the Sins' transport. Mulenear spelling and selected GOW history remain."
  ],
  "Heimdall": [
    [
      11,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "His early confrontation exposes that reading intent cannot verify a sincerely false map. Afterward his independent home search leads him to refuse one patron's repeated lie; no perpetual rematch or unexplained death."
  ],
  "Jörmungandr": [
    [
      4,
      18,
      22,
      27,
      33,
      43,
      45,
      46
    ],
    "The younger battle serpent makes separate coastal choices. Harbors, fishing and resupply change through actual geography and delayed reports. He is not a universal mount or an instant capital rescue."
  ],
  "Deadpool": [
    [
      18,
      24,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Original comedic material follows a failed paid job, a fee dispute and a chosen later bargain. No repeat narrator takeover, copied lines, teleport utility or automatic immortal solution."
  ],
  "Tai Lung": [
    [
      2,
      14,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Pride and desire for earned recognition make him refuse a sponsor's ownership. He can act against a coercive recruiter later for his own reason, not as Daniel's measuring stick or a rescued tame cat."
  ],
  "Maul": [
    [
      12,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Pursues real information about displacement and a power base while recognizing attempts to use him. Works beside the Sins only for bounded benefit. No transported Shadow Collective or source-future knowledge of Starkiller."
  ],
  "Shang Tsung": [
    [
      13,
      18,
      36,
      43,
      44,
      45,
      46
    ],
    "A useful paid treatment earns a buyer before its exploitative price becomes clear. Correspondence and real travel connect his operations. In the finale he preserves his own research/payment interests, losing a real part of the operation."
  ],
  "Riddick": [
    [
      12,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Pursues escape from coercive transport and a route he can use independently. His observations of local terrain are earned. Later assistance buys actual freedom or a debt settled, not enlistment."
  ],
  "Magneto": [
    [
      18,
      33,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Selected X-Men Red-era disappointment and resolve shape his response to Noxian conditional belonging. He builds/protects a chosen local community with actual metal and refuses state ownership. Full issue-level details remain an evidence gap; no late resurrection feats."
  ],
  "Lord Starkiller": [
    [
      13,
      18,
      43,
      44,
      46
    ],
    "Reconstructed coerced servant recognizes a new promise of service. Chooses limited cooperation for maintenance access and autonomy, keeping dark-ending snapshot and custom black sabers without future DLC memories."
  ],

    [
      14,
      17,
      18,
      41,
      43,
      45,
      46
    ],
    "Distinguishes imperial survival from private faction profit. Ravens and reports have real sources. His eventual bargain answers genuine losses; calm strategy is not omniscience or a guarantee he secretly planned every defeat."
  ],
