    [
      13,
      15,
      18,
      33,
      41,
      43,
      44,
      45,
      46
    ],
    "Moves a compromised purchase, loses intermediaries, chooses between rival patrons and cannot recover everything. Deception changes decisions through identifiable channels. No global puppet network or automatic transfer of every Darkin."
  ],

    [
      17,
      18,
      41,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Sion's deployment costs a military route and soldiers. He investigates the private misrepresentation, changes procurement and refuses to confuse destructive power with reliable service. An actual loss changes his judgment."
  ],
  "Draven": [
    [
      14,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],
    "Values applause, profitable spectacle and his own status. A sponsor's failed payment becomes public humiliation. He may abandon an event or ridicule its patron rather than obey because he was paid once."
  ],

    [
      16,
      18,
      43,
      45,
      46
    ],

  ],
  "Talon": [
    [
      16,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],

  ],
  "Riven": [
    [
      12,
      17,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],

  ],

    [
      2,
      14,
      18,
      43,
      46
    ],

  ],

    [
      21,
      33,
      43,
      44,
      45,

    ],

      33,
      43,

      45,
      46

    {
      "identity": "Kayn",
      "source": "character_sources/runeterra_extended/Kayn_Fidelity_Source.txt",
      "status": "PROSPECTIVE_NOT_CURRENT_FACT",
      "role": "Individually motivated participant in the linked conflict below.",
      "motive": "Take the Bloodletters while managing Rhaast and his own ambition.",
      "entry_or_information_route": "The actual weapon trail intersects Yone's hunt for a predatory spiritual disturbance. Kayn independently tries to seize the Bloodletters, while Rhaast wants power and hates competing control. Location: Ionian custody site, spirit threshold and physical forest. SEASON 3 — AUTHOR-APPROVED SBU GANG, FIDELITY, TRAVEL AND CONTINUITY
STATUS: controlling performance specification, not a narrated scene/episode, new duel or accepted event. Latest user directions Oct 8 2026 supersede conflicting performance examples in older modules. All unchanged ENGINE/powers/source mechanics survive.

FAMILY / MONEY / AUTHORITY
The Seven Sins gang is voluntarily chosen family, no automatic boss. Anyone can refuse or leave, the group supports its people first before arguing; admission needs consent of actual current members and Briar is still travelling as NONMEMBER after IV.3. Frank had his exact final III.4 line and no next-morning or later appearance, no rescue, letter, ghost, cameo, POV or gang vote. 'If I got a sandwich we got half a sandwich' and 'Ima eat who I starve with' apply to admitted members of the family; share viable gang scores, food, shelter, goods and gains among them without forced exact per-member formulas. Important individual keepsakes (weapons, Chris's gifted spoon, Billy's banjo/hat) remain personally theirs. No compulsory transfer to Dutch or unrelated nonmembers, no greedy-Dutch manipulative fund, no universal charity to starving civilians, no Charles/Briar default cash share. Outsiders may be friends without becoming Sins. Independent motives and differing judgments persist even among members.

BELLY AND PHYSICAL LOGISTICS
Billy's BELLY OF THE BILLY is the secure high-capacity private warehouse/house/prison controlled by Billy via actual physical swallowing and accessible custody, with its complete existing organic rooms, BOYS CLUB, MEAT CANYON, protected occupants and egress rules. Don't pay an outside warehouse as routine choice if BELLY can lawfully/intentionally take the actual goods. Don't keep equipment on wagons merely to pretend it needs carrying; legitimate wagons can serve stealth, freight acquisition, escape or others' separate cargo. A swallowed prisoner can be held for ransom or under falsely promised ransom as an intentional deception if payer, communication, access, conditions, actor choices and independent pursuit genuinely arise; no automatic ransom/captive. The group normally keeps BELLY's interior layout, capacity and prisoners hush. Only one exterior body, full-size interior other partner, real feed/audio, continuous rapid inverted jaw switch and no healing/gear reset. A new serum did not make Chris cognizant of every Billy change.

MOVEMENT / REST / SIMULATION
AUTHOR TRAVEL CORRECTION, 13 OCTOBER 2026 HONOLULU: the gang does not depend on boats. Choose from its already-earned movement and actual available conveyances before inventing a ship, captain, fare or waiting-for-sailing problem. Boats remain a voluntary option when useful. Kaze can leap and sprint incredibly fast but cannot gain sustained free flight. Billy can run/sprint at his actual speed on land, swim explosively fast in real water, and ferry willing travellers inside BELLY if contact/exterior state allow. Daniel has earned Wukong Nimbus Cloud, subject to its genuine established limitations (no invented duration, flight speed or summon from another reality). Chris's Pokéball-deployed Subaru/X-wing is an existing personal conveyance to consider for ordinary travel, getaways and scores under its full vehicle owner; show physical deployment, occupancy, route and return. Chris and Arthur may also choose safe BELLY passage or actual obtainable modes of transport; Chris's personal experimental aircraft concepts SCRAP-HAWK, IRON MULE, MEOW-RIDER and combat labels SERVICE RUSH/SCRAP SURGEON are merely brainstorms, NOT granted craft/doctrines. Foot travel still has real uses in city/crowds/stealth, no autopiloted hypermovement from every location to every other. Offstage movement requires time/route, environment, visible gear, rest/injury persistence. The weather, possessions, finances, hygiene, prisoners, repairs, witnesses, local reputations and downstream retaliation continue dynamically in the living Red Dead Redemption 2-style world, but never use long infrastructure filler or author-facing accounting as TTS prose. Choose a feasible combination for the actual passengers, cargo and route; safe BELLY passage still requires Billy's real intake and one exterior body. Compress routine travel after orienting the listener. The specific Pokéball deployment/recall is authorized; no general flight, new portal, universal vehicle summon or instant continental crossing is granted.

CHRIS
Real gourmet chef, established RDR2-influenced vocabulary and game activity, Phish fan/tattoo, fabrication mechanic/salvager, gleeful hoarder, relentless culinary similes and profane Theo Von-influenced surreal associative jokes. Maintains exact familiar named weapons, Predator mask, Teemo THE CRITIC input, Dead Eye and serum, Hawkeye-level eyes, elite marksmanship and rapid reactions. When lethal line of sight is available he doesn't deliberately miss or settle for harmless shots to prolong fight; real cover, evasion, range, misperception and shot obstacles still count. His apples/pears allergy is potentially fatal, NO apples or pears prepared/eaten, no secret cure. His chef education of Billy includes mise en place, PHISH arm tattoo, the spoon symbol confused with mother's banjo, and personal original gifted peanut-butter spoon via marsupial pouch. Salvage actual machine/robot donor, physical teardown, tests, failures, practice and correct engineering. In scenes keep GREET GREET ANTAGONIZE, kitchen timing, professional cooking, loot inspection and actual RDR2 range integrated without forced catchphrase cycling or endlessly unrelated references. Corrected Jack death and haunting RDR2 behavior matter. Chris's older pseudo-astral bonding origin is replaced by the psychedelic fusion rare fictional rule.

BILLY
Maintain confident ancient apex ambush, full inherited evolving half-demon half-yordle biology, sources of sensory inputs, lurk/sense/swallow, unnatural banjo/music, Primus/bluegrass, rich Southern hospitality/food bargaining, genuine wicked/affectionate/ignorant sides and huge 110-million-year history. Modern Billy adds Napoleon Dynamite awkward earnestly self-invested displays and Uncle Rico old-athlete nostalgia; his old boasts may refer to actual lived epochs while details may be nonsense. No compulsory catchphrase. Male characters are not routinely called 'sugar' or 'darling' as unintended gendered pet names; allowed if Billy deliberately provokes a man. He has real longtime relationships and outlived much romantic love. His literal original spoon, banjo, revenge hat all persist subject to physical custody. Never treat his immortality as a new ability to give mortal others unconditional survival. Memories cannot grant knowledge of unseen causal details. Brian/Busta and Chris/Billy are two different ontologies, not shared automatic separation and inversion mechanics.

KAZE, DANIEL, ARTHUR
Kaze is shirtless, only samurai pants and black Air Force Ones; intact tattoos and currently retained Sharingan/Black Fire/Shinigami Shrine/Black Marrow full source kit; BLACKCURRENT, Rinnegan and its exchange, and other Kaze League demon packages beyond Nightmare are removed, no reinstated removed Soul Harvest, fake spatial portals, Yasuo wind, shrine radius or unlimited remote swapping. Intelligence includes streetwise gang politics, social threats, manipulation, territorial economics, fame/witness routes and reading a hierarchy. Chaotic but controlled, intense, predatory, thrill-driven: may quickly choose a violent confrontation or enormous landscape-changing destructive attack, including targeted civilians if that fits actual motives; doesn't rely on obligatory after-the-fact moral lectures or spare an opponent because a future card expects it. His real 1v1 signature fights cause ravines, ridge collapse, leveled mountain mass, shoreline disasters or comparable supported geography without fake parity, preserved matching performance proof, durable collateral. At IV.1 he did let Heimdall leave in played history; future portrayals must not use that as a blanket guarantee that he'll always spare a worthy foe.
Daniel full mobility, shuriken, ice, tail, special vision, Nimbus, Wukong truth/troll, no stupidity-as-nerf. all TEN selected solo opponents genuinely die with corpses/consequence, not endless fair fights; Early Cuyler was mud-squid not dog (delivered IV.3 had a wrong narrator description). Arthur original low-honor Red Dead style, own agency/gear, COUNT's earned fully charged shots and sound, cyber arm and eye, Dutch/Jack grief, no assumed permanent obedience to Dutch. Maintain accurate pronunciation in TTS: Mulenear, Yormungander. Exact spelled BONELESS LASAGNA, never generic 'lasagna' when referring to Chris's signature dish. Black Mimir ordinary narrator with distinct old-god intelligence, dry humor and natural listener orientation, except narrowly designated Billy-led prehistoric special.

PAST / FUTURE SOURCE CONTROLS
At IV.3 the Seven Sins have no new accepted member; Briar is following them freely and Frank has departed. Charles Manson's mechanical-fish morph already occurred after five Chris encounters, long-term annoying outsider, no false gang-member rights; his Pepsi, peanut butter, acid-toilet and Hawking remembrance locked lines persist. Heisenberg's established Zaun empire and PURPLE PAIN, Matt's actual distinct route back toward Zaun, Hessa's eighteen gold outstanding, Noxian weapon-sale ban, Thor/Yormungander route, genuine casualty/loot/prisoner logs and all other preexisting effects must not be retroactively 'fixed' by the new special. Gate Aatrox/Fiddlesticks restoration, Zaahen body/chronology and Mordekaiser access as previous owners. Future plan XI.4 remains only prospective. New SBU spec does not rewrite archival Season 2 or enact unperformed main Season 3 events.

PROSE QA / AUDIT
Avoid repeating fake narrator source-validations ('No cartoon recovery'; 'No surprise' etc.), plot-ledger future statements as in-world knowledge, mandatory elaborate TTS scream then baseline whisper, unnatural omniscience, overlong logistics meetings and humor/catchphrase rotation. Continuously sustain voices, chemistry, independent interruptions and realistic circumstances throughout long episodes. The listener should always understand who is present, who is inside BELLY, who knows the information, what each actor wants and the consequence. Character voice influences do not make narrator impersonate the original living artist or borrow copyrighted lines.

Chris's five clashes and their BELLY fallout close the opening arc in III.4, including Frank's one-line final farewell and next-morning absence. Larger remaining duels/Noxian payoffs follow. Brian is miniature and conjoined to full-sized Busta; after the Hawking reveal, gratitude, boast and family roast they leave together. No new PLAY or D173 fact change.

Kaze: Kayn/Rhaast; Fiddlesticks; Yone; Mordekaiser; Zaahen.

Chris: Micah Bell with Bob Ross; adult 1914 Jack Marston; Eyehole Man; apparent Colm O'Driscoll / fictional Stephen Hawking; Mr. Nimbus. Arthur Morgan: Graves; Gangplank; Twisted Fate; Samira; Miss Fortune.
Rek'Sai; Kha'Zix; Nidalee; Skarner; Falkor; Wishbone; Porkchop; a Squidbillies individual (Early Cuyler remains provisional, not user-selected); Meatwad.

There are 45 historical selected positions: 40 still eligible future positions plus five retired Frank positions. No new opponent or duel is created by the Busta/Brian arrival or Charles residency. Chris/Billy are distinct fighters while sharing one exterior. Kayn/Rhaast is one opponent slot with two minds. Divine/support relationships and Teemo remain source-owned, not deleted for a strict '1v1' label. Other long-term duels stay after Chris's opening arc when truly accessible; no guarantee all fit S3.
All regional gates remain: sealed Mordekaiser, unknown independent Aatrox/Fiddlesticks restoration, Zaahen's actual host/history and no Darkin automatic fusion. Twenty-three retained named Darkin exclude Varus/Valmar/Kai deployment; confirmed ancient dead remain historical. No artificial parity, contradictory snapshot, source resurrection or compulsory audience. Chris's selected Bob/Hawking endings and Eyehole near-death are explicit constructed future outcomes, not delivered facts.


FULL RETAINED PAIRING RATIONALE / NOT A SECOND SCHEDULE
KAZE — 5 SIGNATURE MATCHUPS:

  5. Zaahen: Shuriman Ascended/Darkin who voluntarily sealed himself to oppose his own corruption versus Kaze, whose permanent Darkin nature is integrated; Zaahen's status at the branch date and any Xin Zhao host/sacrifice from Riot's later chronology must be independently established, not auto-imported.

CHRIS — 5 SIGNATURE MATCHUPS:
  1. Micah Bell (RDR2 divergent branch): The recognizable aggressive veteran outlaw/traitor and skilled shooter shares the ACTUAL Bob Ross consciousness in one person; meaningful co-conscious influence and control require evidence. Independent arrival and Chris's own real motive remain mandatory. No restored Van der Linde gang; Arthur/Dutch act on their own knowledge.
  2. Jack Marston (RDR1 adult 1914): REUSE legacy jack_marston_1914_adult immediately after Remember My Family / Edgar Ross death. Armed nineteen-year-old son of John and Abigail, grief, books and independent desire. Chris's knowledge of his younger RDR2 self is not automatic full knowledge of 1914.   4. Apparent Colm O'Driscoll: Actually STEPHEN HAWKING IN DISGUISE; the original RDR2 boss is background only. His real personal entry, disguise mechanics and ability to impersonate Colm require evidence. Chris first genuinely hesitates; author-selected future outcome is Chris fatally wounds apparent Colm, then the disguise switches OFF while Hawking is dying, before his final death. Keep identity secret before revelation absent a genuine knowledge route; preserve original Colm's hanging and all others' independent responses.

An established sea grievance and real world-crossing route are required; do not generalize control to every Runeterran Enforcer or invent automatic water on dry land.

  1. Tahm Kench: Two separate supernatural glutton-predators with socially manipulative hospitality; a kinship of demon type is not shared identity/abilities.

DANIEL — 5 SIGNATURE MATCHUPS:
  1. Wukong: Separate Ionian Shimon vastaya Wukong versus Daniel's independent Wukong-linked form/techniques, acrobatics and deception; no shared memories or powers by name.

FRANK CASTLE — 5 SIGNATURE MATCHUPS: [RETIRED FUTURE PERFORMANCE; REFERENCE ONLY]
  1. Lucian: Serious armed hunter driven by personal loss; his supernatural threats, humanity and principled choices can conflict with Frank without forced villainization.

  5. territorial motives and scale are real, not a convenient imposed deathmatch.

LATEST AUTHOR-APPROVED S3 IMMERSION / HYPE ADDITIONS — N01–N14 (7 OCT 2026)
Status: ALL FOURTEEN concepts accepted as selective story-construction possibilities and world-reactivity guidance within the EXISTING fifteen cards. This is an approval of directions, NOT a guarantee each occurs or a lock on its cause, outcome, scene date, character survival, access or previously unwritten knowledge. Do not extend the season to service this list. Prior H01–H12 remain active. Same current endpoint: II.1 D173/R25. Next unwritten IV.1 D174/R26 only on PLAY/NEXT. No played episode revised by this addition.
N01 — PURPLE PAIN VERIFIED CIRCULATION / III–V: The meth–Shimmer product Heisenberg already sells has selected name PURPLE PAIN. When a real buyer, shipment or informant reaches the story, demonstrate a bounded temporary advantage, the dependent user's or worker's own wants, an aftereffect or material loss, and an actual supplier/business consequence. Heisenberg already dominates Zaun crime; do not replay his founding or imply he owns Noxus. Foreign provenance must be established, not inferred from purple coloration.
N02 — COUNTERFEIT PURPLE PAIN / conditional business counterstrand: A rival or opportunist MAY sell imitation product and damage reputation/quality expectations. Identify who could make, distribute and misrepresent it, and show actual circulation/customer perception before Walter knows or retaliates. Not an existing fact, universal recipe or mandatory subplot.
N03 — FAULTY ENEMY FIELD GUIDE / II–IV: A particular hostile witness, official or contractor MAY assemble real Sins observations mixed with false generalizations (especially Kaze's short anchor-swap versus supposed unconstrained teleport, or Billy's swallowing versus assumed digestion). Written copies, messenger delays, tactical changes and failures are real. The enemy adapts competently to its actual evidence and can be right about some limits. No gang access to its unseen plans by default.
N04 — MEAT CANYON/CATFISH PENITENTIARY SOCIAL ECOLOGY / recurring relevant interior: Established prisoners may develop actual grudges, bargains, hierarchies, barter, alliances, and conflicting theories based on what they hear through CLEAR MEAT. Their reactions to Chris's genuine RDR2 play may be comic, hostile or strategic. Do not invent prisoners, breach secure containment, reduce Chris to their universal warden or stage a prison break solely for flavor.
N05 — CHRIS/BILLY BODILY-CHOICE DILEMMA / active major operation: A real moment MAY require exterior precision guns versus Billy's actual swallow/access capability. The partners disagree/choose under the SAME single exterior body; valid time, agency, gear and physical positioning govern what is obtained or lost. Avoid artificial failures from their known capabilities. The switch may be brief, normal or cinematic in narration according to pacing.
N06 — IMMORTAL BASTION HISTORICAL MISREAD / IV–V: A person with real knowledge of Mordekaiser/Black Rose/Darkin history may interpret Kaze's current true Darkin/Black Marrow appearance through mistaken local categories. Knowledge needs a legitimate source or visible evidence, and their conclusion may be right or wrong. No automatically awakened god powers, Kaze/Aatrox merger, restored Aatrox identity or Mordekaiser revival.

N08 — PERSISTENT COMBAT SIGNATURES / all battles: Character-specific damage in buildings, wounds, missing cargo, metal and road structures persists. He remains a nonverbal dog; masking and source-defined range/knowledge limits apply. No subtitles, language translation, convenient omniscient clue or repetitive dog bit.
N10 — KAZE'S UNPREDICTABILITY TACTIC TURNS REAL / Acts III–V: Kaze can consciously amplify Vaas-like unpredictable behavior as outsider-facing psychological warfare, then genuinely change what he wants under provocation, recognition, curiosity, amusement or a destructive appetite. His self-awareness is NOT self-control. This can spoil his own tactical advantage and produce lasting costs; no forced irrationality every scene or universal mind-reading of him.

Track holder, place, condition, provenance, powers and consequences. An unknown artifact doesn't auto-activate; don't acquire an object offscreen for an elegant ending.
N13 — YORMUNGANDER'S COASTAL ECONOMIC AFTERSHOCK / II–V coast strand: Actual fishermen, captains, ports, food supply and travel decisions change in response to the serpent's physical location and observed danger. A supply choke point may be far worse than a large generic treasury loss. Chris, Kaze, Arthur, Daniel and others can dispute retain/sell/destroy decisions with real ownership/access and costs. Avoid inventing a named powerful artifact simply to resolve a later plot.
INTEGRATION PRIORITY: Lean first on N01/N03/N10 and valid N05/N07/N06 spectacle, then use N02/N04/N08/N09/N11/N12/N13/N14 where they naturally intersect existing active conflicts, real people and recorded assets. No extra episode, compulsory cameo, power unlock, resurrection, new seventh Sin, new game save, unearned knowledge, automatic faction hostility or granted future success. AUTHOR-APPROVED HIGH-IMPACT SEASON 3 CONSTRUCTION — 7 OCTOBER 2026, 16:34 HONOLULU
Status: explicitly approved authoring/plot construction DIRECTIONS for existing unwritten Season 3 cards, not performed scenes, forced win conditions, fixed future casualties, source-world facts, or newly accepted episodes. These are now controlling intentions when circumstances support them. Actual outcomes require actors' earned abilities, access, motives, resistance, resources, time and witnessed events. If an earlier PLAY result makes a prospective setup impossible, preserve the result and adapt the idea's purpose locally. Do not revise delivered I.1–II.1 or override D173; the current 21-card master owns all future placement.

H01 — VALUE-CENTERED HEIST / IV.2: Identify and remove the particular stock, payroll, specialized weapons, machinery or transport capacity on which a responsible hostile operation materially depends. The Sins prefer valuable intact seizure when viable; Billy's existing swallowing/secure BELLY storage may strip whole accessible loads. A crippling loss must have demonstrated scale relative to THAT operation, with supply, wages, contracts, protection, workers or patrons reacting; no invented cargo, easy capture, mandatory company collapse or free external storage vulnerability.
H02 — PSYCHOLOGICAL AFTERSHOCK / II–IV: Material loss travels through partial reports into fear, status anxiety, suspicion, internal blame, defections or costly boss decisions. Kaze can sometimes amplify genuine uncertainty; Billy, Arthur and Daniel contribute according to distinct agendas and lawful scene access. Actual mistrust need not be planted or fully controlled by the gang. A frightened leader may learn and counter, not automatically self-destruct.
H03 — HEIMDALL'S CORRECT READ / IV.1: His intent anticipation works visibly through real evasion, counterpressure, Bifrost/sword/Realm Shift as available. Correctly sensing a sincere intention does NOT certify the speaker's claim as objectively true; independently examine any mistaken return-home/access information. Kaze's real and sometimes deliberately presented unpredictability is not a foresight-off switch, guaranteed surprise blow, immunity or infinite social insight. The encounter ends by valid physical reach and competing chosen objectives.
H04 — NO HEAD TO CUT OFF / IV.4 and onward: Outsiders may mistake Kaze for the permanent chief and negotiate, threaten or pay as if he can bind everyone. The gang's real refusal rights and independent acts expose that mistake. Freedom can cost a deal, income, coordination or trust; do not treat lack of hierarchy as effortless advantage, invent formal command, or turn Kaze into a reluctant permanent boss. Arthur/Dutch remains its own genuinely affectionate, independently judged relationship, not a universal leadership lesson.
H05 — HUMILIATION WITH ECONOMIC TEETH / V.1: A hostile patron attempts a public demonstration to restore status after observable losses; the test fails or changes only through actual choices and unequal available abilities. Contracted fighters, voluntary competitors and paying clients each decide what the sponsor's promises mean. The patron's loss of prestige has follow-through in clientele, protection, workforce or bargaining power. This is distinct from I.2's arena-prize event; not a guaranteed second tournament or compulsory death.
H06 — THE VANISHING HOLDING / V.2: An informed attacker expects to destroy the gang's exterior home or stock. If real access, timing and Billy's choice permit, Billy can swallow an entire holding/house or vital structures before they are destroyed. Legitimately stored property stays impenetrably secure through the switch. The attackers may still win LAND, public access, trading relationships, usable outside footing or other exposed stakes. Never conjure a BELLY breach or force the house to disappear merely for a visual trick.
Different Sins can choose different justified responses, with Frank's independent boundaries and Kaze's nonretaliation rule intact. an actual severe offense and responsible-faction notice would be needed for faction-scale retaliation even if that separate rule is later committed.
H08 — THE RANSOM NOBODY WANTS TO PAY / later conditional capture: If the story legitimately captures an individually culpable, personally compromised operative, wealth may make ransom possible but never supplies a captive by itself. Their actual employer, relatives and associates can refuse, betray or be unable to pay. Billy's real prison, admission, custody, release and credible/deceptive bargaining remain governed by their owners. Neither ransom, refusal, family guilt nor the final disposition is fixed here. The broader SBU ransom threshold remains separately staged.
H09 — SION / V.4: Their prospective clash is memorable for warranted mass, battle choices, weapon reach, form-specific mechanics, shattered terrain and durable road/bridge/supply consequences. Source power disparity may shorten the exchange; never invent Sion parity, immortality or a drawn-out survival phase to satisfy spectacle. Darius has independent military priorities.
H10 — WORLD SERPENT / COAST / V: Yormungander's chosen positioning, aggression or restraint and Thor's independent home pursuit change the economic/navigational reality of a SPECIFIC reachable channel or harbor. Shipping routes, escorts, insurance/supply terms and localized survivors respond through actual information routes. No obligatory rematch, unearned translation, sudden home portal, instant capital-to-coast transfer or last-minute rescue of the inland Sins.
H11 — PATRONS FRACTURE THROUGH CHOICE / VI.1–VI.2: Vladimir, Elise, Shang Tsung, LeBlanc/Black Rose and any other actually present patrons hold distinct appetites, assets and limits. As the Sins press a concrete shared capability, one patron's rational resource choice may betray another's expectation. Internal fracture must come from actual misaligned interests and observations; do not summon all named villains to one room, assert omniscience, or label every genuine defeat part of a superior secret plan.
H12 — CONTRADICTORY LEGEND / EVERY ACT: Track different true, false, incomplete or biased witness accounts. Some mistake Arthur for the chief, others focus on Kaze, Billy, Frank, or a particular violent choice. A known witness, actual carrier and receiving interest must change a contract, guarded route, price, pursuit or refusal. No instant universal renown, mandatory viewpoint roll-call, unreliable tale promoted into objective fact, or automatic Death Note identity discovery.

APPROVED CONNECTED CONSEQUENCE CHAIN (ROUTING, NOT GUARANTEED EVENTS): Acts II–III now carry the five Chris encounters and early domestic fallout, while existing Noxian consequences continue through real people. Act IV develops the disputed claim, substantial faction loss, Briar contact and Dutch family friction. Act V makes public humiliation, informed retaliation, Matt's actual return and Sion's route destruction consequential. Act VI attacks the bounded private operation whose patrons can fracture, with coast events independent and an earned price of autonomy. Each phase derives its information, money and access from the previous enacted phase, never from outline knowledge. If one route fails, preserve the defeat and adjust the next feasible action rather than restoring the same convoy, building or boss.

Nobody is spared just to meet a later card. Independent quieter friendship, food, gaming, collection, disagreement and rest must coexist with criminal brutality and actual victims' continuing choices. Older separate pre-/post-SBU Seven Sins staging remains UNCOMMITTED; this batch approves the twelve Season 3 creative applications, not the entire older SBU wholesale.


FULL CARD SUBSTANCE / CURRENT INDICES

S3 I.1 / CARD 01 — R0–4 — THE CLAIM [FULFILLED D148–152; original plan retained as historical guide]
DELIVERED EPISODE: actual outcome and replay/original versions are owned by Current_Continuity_Source. The planning material below is historical, NOT a requirement to redo the scene.
Scene focus / continuing behavior: Begin with arrival needs and the gang choosing how to settle in: food, usable work space, a place to keep what they brought. Wade's brief account of actual paid work is interrupted; Mimir's explicit first-person outrage reclaims narration. The company's evidenced Ambessa connection motivates the existing chosen takeover. During that action Chris finds, actually ignites and obtains the real lightsaber, his FIRST new S3 memorabilia item; the quiet delight afterward matters. Arthur's Dutch recognition is the sole secondary and need not become an instant binding offer. Coastal news reaches Billy through a real local consequence, beginning his grudge. Family life and curiosity precede a new mission list.
New-listener orientation: Establish Noxus as the empire they have sailed to, the inland capital approach and this specific freight holding after the sea/port/inland journey, and the company's evidenced Ambessa trade rather than requiring remembered Arcane politics. Wade is a displaced masked mercenary doing a particular paid job; Dutch is Arthur's old outlaw leader, whose former authority cannot simply resume. Show what a real lightsaber does through its ignition and Chris's recognition. Explain the coastal obstruction as received news, not shared narrator knowledge.
Identify Wade and Deadpool as the same person before switching names; clearly mark the brief change of narrator and Mimir's return. Explain Elsewhered once as displacement from another world. Keep the recap to facts needed for this takeover; do not recite every S2 death. Chris obtains a working energy-bladed weapon, not a Force power.
The Sins identify the private fighting company’s actual connection to Ambessa’s equipment/intimidation trade and choose a profitable act of retaliation. Kaze distinguishes the people responsible from Noxus as a whole, without becoming gentle toward the responsible crew. The takeover is enacted: short, brutal resistance where appropriate, seized usable stock, a real place/stake and somebody left with a loss. Establish who witnesses what.
Deadpool’s brief authorized narration hijack collides with a local who recognizes a consequence of Wade’s own paid work; Mimir resumes. Arthur’s meeting with Dutch is caused by an actual contact or recognition. Dutch is the sole Van der Linde newcomer; his source-world gang does not accompany him. Coastal news of the serpent changes a price or available route during the same operation.
Irreversible turn: the gang has taken something and can now be found through that act. A company survivor/creditor has a concrete reason to retaliate; residents are not unanimously grateful.

S3 I.2 / CARD 02 — R5–6 — WHO GETS THE APPLAUSE [FULFILLED D153–154/R5–6; original plan retained as historical guide]
DELIVERED EPISODE: actual outcome and replay/original versions are owned by Current_Continuity_Source. The planning material below is historical, NOT a requirement to redo the scene.
REQUIRED NEXT OPENING — S3 I.2, AUTHOR CORRECTION 7 OCTOBER 2026, 11:30 HONOLULU
Begin the next episode with a compact, recognizable first-person Mimir orientation to Noxus, before the main offer or conflict takes over. Target roughly 150–220 spoken words, integrated with the actual street/holding and capable of being shorter once the point lands. This is an opening requirement, not new episode prose or a new event.
Explain Noxus as an expansionist empire rather than merely a city or generic villain town. It prizes strength, ambition, demonstrated usefulness and contribution; strength includes strategy, skill and cunning as well as muscle. People from different backgrounds can rise, but that opportunity coexists with conquest, coercion, hierarchy and powerful interests. Imperial ideals are not a guarantee of fairness or every citizen's personal belief. Explain the current capital's martial, crowded, commercially active atmosphere through a few concrete sensory details grounded in the established research and actual location.
Briefly contrast Piltover's wealthy mercantile prestige, invention and polished upper-city presentation with Zaun's industrial undercity, pollution, ingenuity and survival pressures; contrast both with Noxian public pride in strength, military power and advancement through usefulness. These are orientation contrasts, not claims that every inhabitant shares one outlook. The listener should understand why unusual outsiders may find opportunity here and why visible violence or notoriety also attracts recruiters, competitors and authority. The Sins are in the inland capital's freight district, not a coast mislabeled as the capital.
Use Mimir's personality, natural profanity and a sharp observation where it fits, without a lecture, a list of every champion or an omniscient claim about what locals know. Connect directly into the existing I.2 scene. Underlying evidence: reference/research/Noxus_Cast_Fidelity_Research.md, reference/research/Runeterra_Travel_and_Noxus_Geography_Audit.md and topic_sources/Runeterra_World_and_Travel_Source.txt. Research already completed remains usable; do not restart it for this addition.

First-impression application: Use individual, source-supported reactions to the actual Sins present when the offer arrives; Kaze wears only samurai pants and BLACK Air Force Ones. Apply Ensemble_Scene_Application.txt without a reaction roll call. Preserve all existing prize, agency, combat and consequence requirements below. Use expressive exclamation marks where quoted dialogue calls for them.

Scene focus / continuing behavior: Let Draven's offer or interference reach the now-visible gang while they are enjoying or arranging the new holding; they need not go hunting for a tournament. Daniel's skill and recognition exchange with Tai Lung and Samira's independent appetite for danger shape the one prize conflict. A modest game/meal or saber-display moment can frame the decision without a full-cast joke relay. Settle actual gain/loss, witness knowledge and survival before reserving anyone for later.
New-listener orientation: Draven sells arena spectacle and public acclaim; Tai Lung is a displaced martial master seeking the recognition he believes he was denied; Samira is an independent gun-and-blade contractor drawn to danger. Introduce each through the same concrete offer or resistance, not three biography scenes. Name the prize and whose terms bind whom.
Listener-clarity application: Introduce Draven's arena role, Tai Lung's identity as a snow-leopard martial artist, and Samira's paid work through the immediate offer. Explain the actual prize and freedom to refuse before the fight. Recognition, employment and ownership are different stakes.
Draven tries to turn the gang’s public reputation into spectacle under his terms. Kaze sees the implied ownership and negotiates or rejects it; the Sins choose what they stand to gain. Tai Lung acts because recognition matters to him, not because a bracket needs another fighter. Samira’s competing work interferes with the same prize.
Fight focus: a real contest or collision with a definite stake, readable terrain and a supported endpoint. Tai Lung must use his martial skill; a Daniel encounter is a strong proposed matchup only if their objectives and actual kits make it occur. Daniel’s drunken coordination, anatomy and ice remain available; drunkenness is not a blanket foresight immunity. Draven’s axes and crowd craft are dangerous without creating parity with every Sin.
Consequence: a winner gains a specified purse/access/claim, the loser loses it, and a public account becomes exploitable. No automatic friendship after a good fight and no forced tournament arc.

APPROVED H03: Anticipation stays mechanically genuine; a read of intent is not proof the sensed person is right about an alleged way home. Kaze may deliberately menace but does not switch off foresight or obtain a scripted hit.
Scene focus / continuing behavior: One access collision grows from the failed promise. Kaze and Heimdall's disagreement concerns an actual claim, with anticipation succeeding before a supported limitation or decisive counter. Mimir separates remembered fear from observed facts. Chris's excitement about a saber is not Force mastery or a special immunity against Heimdall. No extra import-introduction scene.

Define anticipation by the intention he detects and the physical response it permits; reading a mistaken person's intention does not certify their information. Explain Bifrost or Realm Shift by the visible effect only when used.
Heimdall attempts to secure a person or piece of access relevant to his return to Odin, colliding with the Sins’ own accepted bargain. Show his anticipation through actual movement, counterplay and punishment of readable commitment where his perception and physical capabilities support it; do not guarantee a first-exchange win irrespective of the opponent or situation. Sword, Bifröst and Realm Shift add pressure; he is not an untouchable man standing still while everyone misses.
Proposed central confrontation: Kaze and Heimdall over incompatible claims, with a narrow objective that either can abandon only at a cost. Use current full kits. Do not script an arbitrary hit, a magic blindfold, a compulsory Draupnir imitation, or a mandatory equal duel. If one obtains decisive reach, resolve it. If they break contact, show the actual physical route and why the chosen objective matters more than pursuit.
Turn: his perception correctly detects an immediate intention but does not validate the other person’s mistaken information. The failed promise costs a concrete access/contact. Kaze sees that an apparently useful patron is trying to make the gang enforce someone else’s account. Neither fighter becomes the other’s friend by author decree.

Thor's separate response to the failed home material changes actual access; surviving, available Heimdall acts only on his own information. Collection and comedy can appear around recovery, but the arrival is not a cargo-drop of every wanted relic.
New-listener orientation: Identify the arriving witness by relationship to the actual case and why their survival/consent matters. Talon and Katarina are Noxian assassins with different commitments, not a single assassin faction mind. Thor's failed home lead is the previously offered material, not a newly invented second promise. Matt has physically returned only now.
Listener-clarity application: State what the witness saw or can substantiate, who seeks them, and why. Identify Talon's and Katarina's separate immediate aims and affiliations from their owners without presenting them as a single team. Clearly announce Matt's completed return journey; the witness was not already in Noxus during the crossing.
Matt returns at about R82; his source is now physically available for the first time. Talon’s recovery and Katarina’s pursuit collide with the need to keep that person alive. Distinguish their employers and commitments. The source’s evidence makes the private operation politically expensive, but does not magically compel Swain to trust the gang.
Thor discovers the practical limit of the promised home material and takes the resource/action he considers his due. Heimdall’s own harder pursuit of Odin-related access is separate; neither receives a functioning realm gate. This changes a route the Sins are using and creates an opportunity and a cost.
Consequence: a recovery succeeds or fails definitively, a witness’s status changes through choice, and an access claim is exposed. Do not replay the same false promise and the same escape from IV.1. The evidence is usable because a person survived the route, not because an unearned packet appeared.

S3 V.4 / CARD 18 — R89–98 — RELEASE THE WRONG THING [CLIMAX IV]
no artificial equal fight.

There is no Frank/Darius overlap: Frank has already left permanently and cannot appear again. Darius and the present Sins already have their own separate reasons to act. Give the spectacle an intelligible beginning, strongest physical change and aftermath; name fatalities. Do not insert a new collecting errand or team move rotation. Let the wrecked route shape later food, travel and repair needs.
New-listener orientation: Sion is a deployed reanimated Noxian war weapon with destructive momentum, not an ordinary private guard. Darius commands for imperial interests and can oppose the private diversion without joining the Sins. Explain the threatened route and observable axe/impact effects.
Listener-clarity application: Explain Sion's reanimated state through action before relying on unusual injury response. Distinguish Darius's imperial deployment from the private company's interference. Establish relative positions before the largest impact and explicitly identify any resulting death, survival or uncertain fate.
Sion’s imperial deployment meets a route already destabilized by private interference and the Sins’ retaliation. Darius’s actual command interest diverges from the private patrons. Sion threatens the objective and people of more than one side; no one can safely pretend he is a professional guard taking precise private orders.
The fight may end in a few decisive exchanges if the power relation warrants it. Its memorable quality comes from impact, terrain change, choice and aftermath, not forced endurance. Riven’s participation is her decision. Nobody queues up to repeat the same attack.
Payoff/cost: the deployment cannot be used again unchanged; the affected route/site suffers lasting damage. Darius has evidence to act against the private diversion without becoming the Sins’ ally. New evidence can strengthen the surviving cross-border investigation only when a legitimate report reaches a living, established recipient who chooses to act. Caitlyn is dead in the revised S2 endpoint; neither her command nor her personal relationships transfer automatically. A dispatch here is not same-scene receipt in Zaun. The main notebook remains with Heisenberg under the planned S2 custody chain. No restored book or cancelled cure.

Reintroduce helpers through their current bargain rather than a list of franchises. Explain any consequential unfamiliar collectible by its demonstrated function and current holder.
The Sins choose the final operation for their own grievance, recovery and profit. The scope is explicit: break the particular works’ coercive capacity, recover what they actually claim, and deny the patrons the means of repeating the attack. They do not sign up to liberate all Noxus. Planning and refusal occupy only what is needed; the assault starts in this episode.
If alive and actually available after earlier action, Maul offers an opening with a claim attached; the gang refuses the dependency, forcing a changed route and a real cost. Only living, actually available participants retain these later functions: Riddick can negotiate a specific passage, Samira a defined division of work, and Deadpool can choose to stay through danger. Turn: the operation commits resources and forfeits an escape from the feud. The first objective is actually taken or destroyed. Chris’s salvage choice, Daniel’s deception, Arthur’s current weapon conditions and the family’s mutual knowledge matter through function, not a move checklist.

The gang’s refusal is tested by an actual collection/enforcement attempt or enforceable loss, not just a final-room speech. Thor still has his own relationship to the failed home lead. The Sins’ chosen mercenary identity follows what they actually did and refused. End on a completed consequence and a chosen direction; no mandatory Season 4 teaser replaces resolution.


FIGHT / CONSEQUENCE CONTRACT
Every consequential fight needs a motive, a physical place, a knowable objective, actual current abilities/equipment, an outcome and a changed next scene. Use the quickest supported decisive action. An epic fight can be brief; an epic episode can contain several uneven fights, reversals and hard choices. Do not turn the requested set pieces into survival guarantees, forced pairings, repeated escape scripts or immunity tests. A protected protagonist need not remain unscratched, prosperous, respected or morally vindicated. No cost is invented solely because every power supposedly needs a downside.

# Season 3 — finalized condensed main-story outline

26 original remaining cards become 16 staged episodes, 18–33. Completed main Episodes 1–17 stay intact; five approved Billy Specials are separate. Count follows consequential pacing, not a quota.

One opening title in eventual narration, no internal mini-titles or scene labels. These outline headings are navigation only. Verified Vecna profile controls powers, creatures and cosmology; prior speculative mechanics are retired.

## 18. What the Label Hides — VI.3, VI.4

# Approved Season 3 portrayal corrections — 17 October 2026 Honolulu

Author-approved configuration, not new events. Applies to the active reset after delivered E17. Preserve the exact episodes, E16 Revised, Seasons 1–2 and retained campfire evidence. Current reconciliation overrides old branch cursors. No incoming character, camera discovery, completed repair, healed injury or relationship is created by this document.

## Chris: recovery and practical repair

Chris's already-earned serum and healing inside BELLY must produce noticeably accelerated recovery, not ordinary-human injury persistence. He remains slower to heal than Kaze. At each meaningful elapsed-time transition, reassess the actual injury: bleeding, wound closure, pain, mobility and functional limitation need not recover at identical speeds. Carry forward only impairment still supported by severity and elapsed recovery. Do not repeat the same limp, pain or incapacity for several episodes merely because it was once recorded. Switching alone is not a cure. A wound sustained seconds ago still matters; no fixed numerical recovery schedule, resurrection or new human-exterior limb regrowth is granted.

Chris is an effective MacGyver-like improvisational engineer, not a helpless owner of perpetually broken gadgets. He repairs familiar, ordinary damage promptly at his established BELLY workbench with accessible tools and compatible salvage. A loose mount, broken housing, ordinary wiring fault or familiar mechanical repair is normally brief practical work, not a multi-episode procurement subplot. Let the fix work. Ugly, asymmetric, taped, bolted or repurposed construction can be competent and dependable; every improvised repair does not need a later failure joke.

Show the minimum useful causal chain: identify what failed, use an actually available compatible part or adjustment, perform a functional check, and update the item's condition. This can occupy a few sentences alongside conversation. Do not deliver real-world construction instructions for weapons. A field patch and restored full function are distinct only when the actual repair leaves a limitation; do not manufacture a second repair appointment. Familiar repair does not require a new invention unlock or displace the two approved future-project priorities.

An unfamiliar alien core, destroyed unique component, missing essential material or inaccessible item may require more work. State that specific obstruction, not a generic 'damaged equipment' tag. Chris can salvage an understandable housing or connection without magically mastering the device's whole technology. Repairs restore supported functions, not new powers, infinite ammunition or duplicated Teemo. His current cowl/gear remains as delivered at E17 until the next story actually establishes the repair; the patch does not retroactively edit the manuscript.

## Daniel: accelerated simian recovery — retained by later rework

The author-approved markedly faster recovery remains a project-owned mythic-simian trait after the later 18 October 2026 UTC identity rework. The former Saiyan-hybrid explanation is superseded; the separately approved recovery is retained. This is not a claim that real primates regenerate. Treat ordinary cuts, bruising, soreness and exertion as recovering substantially faster than an ordinary human's when actual time passes. His Tai Lung injuries are not a permanent handicap or an excuse to bench him indefinitely.

Severe trauma still has its actual immediate effect and requires supported recovery. No instant whole-body reset, resurrection, severed-limb regrowth, intoxication cleansing, automatic near-death power boost or borrowed Wukong immortality layers are granted. Ice anatomy, clone transfer, transformation and bottle replenishment do not independently heal him. Record improvement when elapsed time supports it; do not silently erase an acute injury or preserve a stale restriction after it should have improved. Exact rates remain qualitative; do not invent a stopwatch or force a medical subplot.

## Chris and Kaze: craft instruction and earned frustration

Use the approved Big Z/Cody board-making relationship dynamic: tactile knowledge, respecting how material behaves, a teacher absorbed in the process, and a capable learner who wants the result and is becoming thoroughly sick of the teaching. Chris can demonstrate something genuinely useful, repeat an instruction because Kaze is forcing a task, digress into a bizarre analogy, or take over while insisting he is not taking over. Kaze can try, object, mock the method, rush, refuse or concede that the technique works. The humor comes from their particular conflict over pace and control, not making either stupid.

This is not a copied Surf's Up scene, mandatory quotation, surfer accent or new Chris biography. Kaze retains existing mastery and copying limits; he is not made incompetent at a task he knows. Chris's expertise is task-specific rather than universal superiority. Let shared work supply affection without requiring a sentimental explanation. Use at a chosen meaningful craft/cooking task, not every repair. Routine gear fixes remain quick; teacher frustration must not become another reason to strand the gang over maintenance.

## Manson & Meatwad's Inside Job

Approved series title: **Manson & Meatwad's Inside Job**. Their self-important production label is **Inside Job Productions**. This is a BELLY-only in-world mockumentary: Surf's Up's informal documentary framing, The Office US's self-presentation versus observed behavior, and Trailer Park Boys' improvised domestic scheming. Trailer Park Boys influence belongs here, not TPP. These are broad comic methods, not copied dialogue or universal personalities.

Charles and Meatwad discover the camera while digging through Chris's hoarded junk. Chris recognizes it as his and objects to them appropriating it. Establish its actual usable recording capability economically; no camera has been found merely because this patch exists. No unearned magical power, network, exterior drone, invisible crew or public broadcast. Recording, playback and an actual audience are separate facts. Billy's live exterior monitor is not automatic stored mockumentary footage.

When people enter BOYS CLUB, they may encounter the two filming, be addressed by them and be invited into their ridiculous project. Entry does not compel participation or surrender privacy. A person may refuse, interrupt, conceal something, walk away or turn the question around. Their real relationships and boundaries decide the response. Women are not excluded from BOYS CLUB. Existing intake, containment, private storage and one-exterior-body rules remain intact.

Charles and Meatwad alternate cameraman and interviewer, sometimes switching because each wants to be in the shot. Establish who is holding the camera when it matters. Charles wants importance, admiration, authorship and control; Meatwad is genuinely interested in people and in making something together. Both can be foolish, insightful, cooperative or annoyed. Charles's literacy limits remain; the project does not appoint him warden or give him authority over residents.

Chris is often over these two roommates, but is not permanently their exhausted parent. He can refuse, answer while working, become reluctantly interested, seize the interview to explain a project, or willingly enjoy showing off. His concentration, chef standards, fabrication competence, curiosity and absurd associations remain. Sometimes the interview keeps going because Chris won't stop explaining. Charles and Meatwad can enjoy or regret getting exactly what they asked for.

Cut to selected personal interviews after a scene when a different interpretation, lie, motive, unexpected honesty or contradiction improves it. Do not recap every event or interrupt every action with a confessional. Spoken orientation must identify the changed place/time and speaker clearly enough for audio-only listening. Black Mimir remains narrator; interviews are situated speech, not replacement omniscient narration. Footage records only what this camera actually sees/hears. No filmed exterior adventure or actor knowledge acquired merely from an editorial cut. These interviews occur inside BELLY, even when an informed guest discusses a real exterior experience.

Retained approved comic repertoire, optional and consequence-sensitive:

1. An interview about giving interviews turns Charles's coaching back on him.
2. Chris accidentally gives the longest interview after claiming he has no time.
3. A supposedly private confessional is audible to Chris working nearby; actual room sound matters.
4. People prefer talking to Meatwad; Charles appoints himself executive producer and intrudes.
5. Arthur refuses an interview while continuing his task, then finds himself answering anyway; refusal can also stand.
6. Kaze's curiosity turns the questions on Charles; no automatic violence or compulsory intimidation win.
7. Charles's official tour claims credit for things and gets corrected by people who actually know.
8. A production meeting is already recording, catching an argument over what they should pretend happened.
9. Apparently boring footage contains a genuinely captured small problem; not magic surveillance or a manufactured security breach.
10. An imperfect take gives somebody sincere credit and they keep it, rather than always ruining warmth with a joke.

No episode quota, forced response, perpetual canned reaction, mandatory laugh, new biography or guaranteed success. Existing approved household repertoire remains available wherever consistent. Safeguard secure BELLY storage: the camera premise is authorized discovery/use of an ordinary object, not a new hostile theft vulnerability.

## TPP: strengthen distinct engines, keep consequences

TPP remains Tampon Pandemic Productions: Dutch, adult Clifford and one conjoined Busta/Brian body, four minds in three bodies. Keep the established Brusta Thymes identity; no separate Brian or replacement Busta. Preserve late-Chapter-3 Dutch, Bob's twin relationship and sincere grief, unfinished Tahiti painting, Clifford's actual mobility limits and TP humiliation, and current rent/cash facts. The murder and tattoos happened E17, not future repeat scenes.

Use the approved Prestige Worldwide self-promotion, Brian's Dr. Evil grandiosity against Busta's Scotty-like practical objections, Anchorman rival-crew status clashes, Spaceballs theatrical embarrassment, South Park absurd self-importance and Dutch's actual RDR2 psychology. Never turn everyone into the same loud idiot. Busta can be competent and cruel, not merely Brian's corrective parent. Dutch's persuasion can genuinely succeed. Brian can claim someone else's effective idea; others may challenge him. Clifford remains a dangerous person, not harmless slapstick.

Let an earned modest success remain a success and make them insufferably pleased with it. Vanity spending, presentation and rival claims then create particular consequences. The landlord has ordinary motives and actual leverage, not magical immunity or a standing babysitter role. Real attachment, cooperation, grief and seriousness can coexist with ridiculous behavior. Existing full approved TPP options remain in the outline; no compulsory gag rotation or forced loss to protect the Sins.

It consolidates first-encounter adult initiative and the recovered Isolde-era ongoing-relationship behavior, preserving current agency and non-graphic presentation. ## Scene preparation and whole-draft review integration

Use the installed storytelling workflow, not an additional parallel procedure. Retrieve full relevant character owners plus this scoped correction for healing, repairs, craft teaching, household filming or TPP scenes. For a fight retrieve the existing kit map and opponent fidelity research. Preserve Kaze's danger perception, living Black Marrow, retained Sharingan/Black Fire, Nightmare, Shrine and independently retained packages under the latest Kaze removals; Cleave and World-Cutting Slash remain sword-only. Unknown retained reward details are not revoked or invented.

During the existing complete-draft review, check for stale injury/equipment tags, unnecessary repair delays, identical reactions across characters, camera omniscience, forced interview participation, repeated completed TPP setups and generic spectacle without readable positions or consequences. Repair actual passages and reread the whole revision. Preserve the current 25–30-minute target and established minimum without padding; structural checks do not certify performance quality. No episode is generated or retrospectively revised by this WORK patch.
